              rows
            );
            const key = submissionKey('b1', 'morning', tomorrowIso);
            const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG', {
              hour: '2-digit',
              minute: '2-digit',
            });
            const msgId = `msg_tour_${Date.now()}`;
            const msg: ChatMessage = {
              id: msgId,
              branchId: 'b1',
              sender: 'branch',
              kind: 'availability',
              text: formatAvailabilityChatText(
                branch.name,
                'morning',
                tomorrowIso,
                rows,
                compliance,
                branch.emps
              ),
              shift: 'morning',
              shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
              submissionKey: key,
              time: timeNow,
              readByAdmin: false,
              readByBranch: true,
            };
            return {
              ...prev,
              submissions: {
                ...prev.submissions,
                [key]: {
                  key,
                  branchId: 'b1',
                  shift: 'morning',
                  shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
                  submittedAt: timeNow,
                  rows,
                  compliance,
                },
              },
              messages: [...prev.messages, msg],
              notifications: [
                ...prev.notifications,
                {
                  id: `ntour_${Date.now()}`,
                  kind: compliance.status === 'matched' ? 'compliance_ok' : 'compliance_diff',
                  senderRole: 'branch',
                  senderName: 'فرع الصنادلي',
                  recipientRole: 'central',
                  branchId: 'b1',
                  shift: 'morning',
                  shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
                  messageId: msgId,
                  text: `إرسال فرع الصنادلي (صباحي - غداً) مطابق للخطة الأسبوعية (${compliance.actualCount} متاح)`,
                  time: timeNow,
                  read: false,
                  complianceReport: compliance,
                },
              ],
              audit: [
                ...prev.audit,
                {
                  id: Date.now(),
                  time: timeNow,
                  text: `إرسال توفر الغد (الوردية الصباحية): فرع الصنادلي — مطابق للخطة الأسبوعية`,
                },
              ],
            };
          });
          setSession((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            role: 'central',
            branchId: 'b1',
            workspace: 'availability',
            adminScreen: 'control',
            adminShift: 'morning',
          }));
        },
      },
      {
        id: 't_admin_shifts',
        title: '6. شاشتك يا أستاذ همام — زرارين كبار للصباحي والمسائي',
        text: 'دول زراير الورديات الكبيرة للموبايل عشان متتوهش: تقلّب بين الصباحي والمسائي في لمسة وتشوف العجز بالأحمر، الفائض بالأصفر، والمستقر بالأخضر.',
        targetId: 'adminShiftButtons',
        onEnter: () => {
          setSession((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            role: 'central',
            branchId: 'b1',
            workspace: 'availability',
            adminScreen: 'control',
            adminShift: 'morning',
          }));
        },
      },
      {
        id: 't_admin_notif',
        title: '7. الإشعار يوديك للحدث نفسه علطول',
        text: 'أي إشعار يوصلك هنا من أي فرع وتضغط عليه، بيدخلك فوراً على شات الفرع وعلى الرسالة نفسها بالظبط من غير ما تدور.',
        targetId: 'notifBellBtn',
        onEnter: () => {
          setSession((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            role: 'central',
            branchId: 'b1',
            workspace: 'availability',
            adminScreen: 'control',
            adminShift: 'morning',
          }));
        },
      },
      {
        id: 't_admin_control',
        title: '8. شات الفروع + نقل فوري + تم الاطلاع',
        text: 'اضغط على أي فرع تفتح شاته، تراجع خطته للقراءة فقط، أو تعمل «نقل موظف» من فرع فيه فائض لفرع فيه عجز. ولما تضغط «تم الاطلاع» العلامة بتتقلب أزرق عند الفرع.',
        targetId: 'adminBranchCard_b1',
        onEnter: () => {
          setSession((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            role: 'central',
            branchId: 'b1',
            workspace: 'availability',
            adminScreen: 'control',
            adminShift: 'morning',
          }));
        },
      },
      {
        id: 't_final',
        title: 'كده كله تمام يا أستاذ همام!',
        text: 'دلوقتي تقدر تشتغل علطول على الموبايل برمزك (0000) أو تجرب أي فرع (12341–12348). ولو احتجت تفتح الشرح في أي وقت، اضغط على «الدليل» فوق.',
        isFinal: true,
      },
    ],
    [tomorrowIso]
  );

  const currentBranch =
    state.branches.find((b) => b.id === session.branchId) || state.branches[0];

  // For Branch Desktop Sidebar summary
  const branchUnreadFromAdmin = useMemo(() => {
    if (session.role !== 'branch') return 0;
    return state.messages.filter(
      (m) => m.branchId === currentBranch.id && m.sender === 'admin' && !m.readByBranch
    ).length;
  }, [state.messages, session.role, currentBranch.id]);

  const branchLastMessage = useMemo(() => {
    if (session.role !== 'branch') return null;
    const list = state.messages.filter((m) => m.branchId === currentBranch.id);
    return list.length > 0 ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
  }, [state.messages, session.role, currentBranch.id]);

  return (
    <>
      {showOnboarding ? (
        <OnboardingModal
          onSkip={() => setShowOnboarding(false)}
          onStartTour={() => {
            setShowOnboarding(false);
            setTourOpen(true);
          }}
        />
      ) : !session.role ? (
        /* WhatsApp-Styled Login Screen */
        <div className="wa-chat-bg flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 py-8">
          <div
            id="loginCard"
            className="w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white shadow-lg"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-[#008069] px-6 py-5 text-white">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15">
                <MessageCircle className="h-6 w-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-lg font-extrabold">Roast Shift · واتساب الشفتات</h1>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/85">
                  أدخل رمز الأدمن ({state.adminPin}) أو رمز فرعك للفتح
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6">
              <form
                onSubmit={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  handleLogin();
                }}
              >
                <div className="relative mb-4">
                  <input
                    type={showPin ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    value={pinInput}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      if (loginError) setLoginError('');
                      setPinInput(normalizeDigits(e.target.value));
                    }}
                    placeholder="••••"
                    dir="ltr"
                    className="font-mono-num w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] py-3 pl-11 pr-11 text-center text-2xl font-bold tracking-[0.4em] text-[#111b21] outline-none focus:border-[#008069]"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPin((v) => !v)}
                    aria-label={showPin ? 'إخفاء الرمز' : 'إظهار الرمز'}
                    className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-md p-1 text-[#667781] hover:text-[#111b21]"
                  >
                    {showPin ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2" dir="ltr">
                  {['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', 'مسح', '0', '←'].map((k) => (
                    <button
                      key={k}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (loginError) setLoginError('');
                        if (k === 'مسح') setPinInput('');
                        else if (k === '←') setPinInput((p) => p.slice(0, -1));
                        else setPinInput((p) => p + k);
                      }}
                      className="font-mono-num min-h-[46px] rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] py-2.5 text-base font-bold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]"
                    >
                      {k}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                {loginError && (
                  <div className="mb-3 text-center text-xs font-bold text-rose-600">
                    {loginError}
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="min-h-[48px] w-full rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-3 text-sm font-extrabold text-white shadow-xs hover:bg-[#016b58]"
                >
                  فتح المحادثات
                </button>
              </form>

              {/* Quick Demo Strip */}
              <div className="mt-6 border-t border-[#e9edef] pt-4">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowOnboarding(true)}
                  className="mb-3 flex min-h-[42px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] hover:bg-[#d9fdd3]"
                >
                  <Compass className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                  <span>الدليل التعريفي للأستاذ همام (شرح المزايا في دقيقة)</span>
                </button>

                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                  <span>دخول سريع للتجربة:</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setTourOpen(true)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 font-extrabold text-[#008069] hover:underline"
                  >
                    <Compass className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>جولة عملية مباشرة</span>
                  </button>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleLogin(state.adminPin)}
                  className="mb-2 min-h-[40px] w-full rounded-xl border border-[#86efac] bg-[#e7fce3] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] hover:bg-[#d9fdd3]"
                >
                  الأدمن المركزي — واتساب الفروع ({state.adminPin})
                </button>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                  {state.branches.map((b) => (
                    <button
                      key={b.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleLogin(b.pin)}
                      className="min-h-[36px] rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-right text-[11px] font-bold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]"
                    >
                      {b.name} <span className="font-mono-num text-[#667781]">({b.pin})</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-end text-[11px] text-[#667781]">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const fresh = createSeedState();
                      setState(fresh);
                      notify('تمت إعادة تعيين المحادثات التجريبية');
                    }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-rose-700"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" />
                    <span>تصفير التجربة</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-[100dvh] bg-[#eae6df] pb-6 text-[#111b21]">
          {/* Authentic WhatsApp Top Green Header with Prominent 3-Tab Navigation */}
          <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#008069] text-white shadow-sm">
            <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 sm:px-6">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15">
                  <MessageCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="truncate text-base font-extrabold leading-tight">
                    Roast Shift
                  </div>
                  <div className="truncate text-[11px] font-semibold text-white/85">
                    {session.role === 'central'
                      ? 'الأدمن المركزي · مراجعة الفروع'
                      : `مدير فرع ${currentBranch.name}`}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Desktop Navigation: Admin has 2 fast tabs; Branch has 3 tabs */}
              <nav className="hidden items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-black/15 p-1 md:flex">
                <button
                  id="navAvailabilityTab"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    changeSession({
                      workspace: 'availability',
                      branchScreen: 'home',
                      adminScreen: 'control',
                    })
                  }
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                    session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                    (session.role === 'central'
                      ? session.adminScreen === 'control'
                      : session.branchScreen !== 'settings')
                      ? 'bg-white text-[#008069]'
                      : 'text-white/90 hover:bg-white/10'
                  }`}
                >
                  <MessageCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>
                    {session.role === 'central' ? 'الفروع والمحادثات' : 'المحادثة والغد'}
                  </span>
                </button>

                {session.role === 'branch' && (
                  <button
                    id="navPlannerTab"
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      changeSession({ workspace: 'planner' });
                      setPlannerTabReq((r) => ({ tab: 'hub', nonce: (r?.nonce || 0) + 1 }));
                    }}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                      session.workspace === 'planner'
                        ? 'bg-white text-[#008069]'
                        : 'text-white/90 hover:bg-white/10'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <CalendarDays className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>خطة الأسبوع</span>
                  </button>
                )}

                <button
                  id="navSettingsTab"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    changeSession({
                      workspace: 'availability',
                      adminScreen: 'settings',
                      branchScreen: 'settings',
                    })
                  }
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                    session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                    (session.role === 'central'
                      ? session.adminScreen === 'settings'
                      : session.branchScreen === 'settings')
                      ? 'bg-white text-[#008069]'
                      : 'text-white/90 hover:bg-white/10'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Settings className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>{session.role === 'central' ? 'الإعدادات' : 'طاقم الفرع'}</span>
                </button>
              </nav>

              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
                <button
                  id="notifBellBtn"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setNotifOpen(true)}
                  className="relative flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-white/25"
                >
                  <Bell className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">الإشعارات</span>
                  {unreadCount > 0 && (
                    <span className="font-mono-num rounded-full bg-[#25d366] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                      {unreadCount}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTourOpen(true)}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#25d366] px-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#1fc45b]"
                >
                  <Compass className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">الدليل</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="flex h-9 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-white/25"
                  title="تسجيل الخروج"
                >
                  <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">خروج</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Mobile Tab Strip (Zero Emojis; 2 Tabs for Admin, 3 for Branch) */}
            <div
              className={`grid border-t border-white/15 text-center text-xs font-extrabold md:hidden ${
                session.role === 'central' ? 'grid-cols-2' : 'grid-cols-3'
              }`}
            >
              <button
                id="navAvailabilityTabMobile"
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  changeSession({
                    workspace: 'availability',
                    branchScreen: 'home',
                    adminScreen: 'control',
                  })
                }
                className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border-b-3 py-2.5 transition-colors ${
                  session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                  (session.role === 'central'
                    ? session.adminScreen === 'control'
                    : session.branchScreen !== 'settings')
                    ? 'border-[#25d366] text-white'
                    : 'border-transparent text-white/75'
                }`}
              >
                <MessageCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{session.role === 'central' ? 'الفروع' : 'المحادثة'}</span>
              </button>

              {session.role === 'branch' && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    changeSession({ workspace: 'planner' });
                    setPlannerTabReq((r) => ({ tab: 'hub', nonce: (r?.nonce || 0) + 1 }));
                  }}
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border-b-3 py-2.5 transition-colors ${
                    session.workspace === 'planner'
                      ? 'border-[#25d366] text-white'
                      : 'border-transparent text-white/75'
                  }`}
                >
                  <CalendarDays className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>الخطة</span>
                </button>
              )}

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  changeSession({
                    workspace: 'availability',
                    adminScreen: 'settings',
                    branchScreen: 'settings',
                  })
                }
                className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border-b-3 py-2.5 transition-colors ${
                  session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                  (session.role === 'central'
                    ? session.adminScreen === 'settings'
                    : session.branchScreen === 'settings')
                    ? 'border-[#25d366] text-white'
                    : 'border-transparent text-white/75'
                }`}
              >
                {session.role === 'central' ? (
                  <>
                    <Settings className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>الإعدادات</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Users className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>الطاقم</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </header>

          {/* Main Content */}
          <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-2 pt-3 sm:px-6 sm:pt-4">
            {session.role === 'branch' ? (
              /* Branch Desktop WhatsApp Web 2-Pane Frame */
              <div className="grid items-start gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[300px_1fr]">
                {/* Right WhatsApp Chats Sidebar for Branch (Desktop) */}
                <aside className="hidden overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white shadow-sm lg:block">
                  <div className="border-b border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#54656f]">
                    أقسام فرع {currentBranch.name}
                  </div>
                  <div className="divide-y divide-[#e9edef]">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        changeSession({ workspace: 'availability', branchScreen: 'home' })
                      }
                      className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 text-right transition-colors ${
                        session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                        session.branchScreen !== 'settings'
                          ? 'bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                          : 'hover:bg-[#f8fafc]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-white">
                        <Building2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <span className="truncate text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                            المدير المركزي
                          </span>
                          <span className="font-mono-num text-[10px] text-[#54656f]">
                            {branchLastMessage?.time || 'الآن'}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <span className="truncate text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                            {branchLastMessage
                              ? branchLastMessage.text.split('\n')[0]
                              : 'إرسال توفر ورديات الغد'}
                          </span>
                          {branchUnreadFromAdmin > 0 ? (
                            <span className="font-mono-num flex h-5 min-w-[20px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#25d366] px-1.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-white">
                              {branchUnreadFromAdmin}
                            </span>
                          ) : branchLastMessage?.sender === 'branch' ? (
                            <CheckCheck
                              className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 ${
                                branchLastMessage.readByAdmin
                                  ? 'text-[#53bdeb]'
                                  : 'text-[#667781]'
                              }`}
                            />
                          ) : null}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        changeSession({ workspace: 'planner' });
                        setPlannerTabReq((r) => ({ tab: 'hub', nonce: (r?.nonce || 0) + 1 }));
                      }}
                      className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 text-right transition-colors ${
                        session.workspace === 'planner' ? 'bg-[#f0f2f5]' : 'hover:bg-[#f8fafc]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#008069]">
                        <CalendarDays className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                          خطة الأسبوع
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                          لوحة اليوم والجدول · الموظفون والإجازات
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        changeSession({ workspace: 'availability', branchScreen: 'settings' })
                      }
                      className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 text-right transition-colors ${
                        session.workspace === 'availability' &&
                        session.branchScreen === 'settings'
                          ? 'bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                          : 'hover:bg-[#f8fafc]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-700">
                        <Users className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                          طاقم الفرع
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                          {currentBranch.emps.filter((e) => !e.suspended).length} موظف مسجل
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </aside>

                {/* Active Branch Workspace */}
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  {session.workspace === 'planner' ? (
                    <PlannerModule
                      state={state}
                      branchId={currentBranch.id}
                      isAdmin={false}
                      externalTab={plannerTabReq}
                      onSelectBranch={(bId) => changeSession({ branchId: bId })}
                      onUpdateState={updateState}
                      onNotify={notify}
                      onGoToAvailability={(sh: ShiftType) =>
                        changeSession({
                          workspace: 'availability',
                          branchScreen: 'submit',
                          activeShift: sh,
                        })
                      }
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <BranchAvailabilityView
                      state={state}
                      session={session}
                      branch={currentBranch}
                      focusEvent={focusEvent}
                      onChangeSession={changeSession}
                      onUpdateState={updateState}
                      onNotify={notify}
                      onOpenPlanner={() => {
                        changeSession({ workspace: 'planner' });
                        setPlannerTabReq((r) => ({ tab: 'hub', nonce: (r?.nonce || 0) + 1 }));
                      }}
                    />
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : session.workspace === 'planner' ? (
              <PlannerModule
                state={state}
                branchId={currentBranch.id}
                isAdmin={true}
                externalTab={plannerTabReq}
                onSelectBranch={(bId) => changeSession({ branchId: bId })}
                onUpdateState={updateState}
                onNotify={notify}
                onBackToAdminChats={() =>
                  changeSession({ workspace: 'availability', adminScreen: 'control' })
                }
              />
            ) : (
              <AdminControlView
                state={state}
                session={session}
                selectedCompliance={selectedCompliance}
                focusEvent={focusEvent}
                onCloseComplianceModal={() => setSelectedCompliance(null)}
                onSelectCompliance={(rep) => setSelectedCompliance(rep)}
                onChangeSession={changeSession}
                onUpdateState={updateState}
                onNotify={notify}
                onOpenBranchPlanner={(bId) =>
                  changeSession({ branchId: bId, workspace: 'planner' })
                }
              />
            )}
          </main>

          {/* Notifications Modal */}
          {notifOpen && (
            <div
              className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
              onClick={() => setNotifOpen(false)}
            >
              <div
                className="max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-lg overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
              >
                <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-3">
                  <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                    الإشعارات والرسائل الواردة
                  </h3>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setNotifOpen(false)}
                    className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold"
                  >
                    إغلاق
                  </button>
                </div>

                {myNotifs.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-[#667781]">
                    لا توجد إشعارات حتى الآن.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    {[...myNotifs].reverse().map((n) => (
                      <button
                        key={n.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleNotificationClick(n)}
                        className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-3.5 text-right transition-colors hover:border-[#008069] ${
                          !n.read
                            ? n.kind === 'compliance_ok'
                              ? 'border-[#86efac] bg-[#e7fce3]'
                              : n.kind === 'compliance_diff' || n.kind === 'emergency'
                              ? 'border-rose-300 bg-rose-50'
                              : 'border-amber-300 bg-amber-50'
                            : 'border-[#e9edef] bg-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#111b21]">
                            {n.kind === 'compliance_ok' && (
                              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                            )}
                            {(n.kind === 'compliance_diff' || n.kind === 'emergency') && (
                              <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 text-rose-700" />
                            )}
                            <span>{n.senderName}</span>
                            {n.isEdit && (
                              <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-amber-900">
                                تعديل
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </span>
                          <span className="font-mono-num text-[11px] font-normal text-[#667781]">
                            {n.time}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-semibold leading-relaxed text-[#111b21]">
                          {n.text}
                        </p>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                          <span>
                            {SHIFTS_AR[n.shift]} · {fmtDateAr(n.shiftDate)}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[#008069]">اضغط للانتقال للحدث</span>
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Interactive Tour */}
      <InteractiveTour
        open={tourOpen}
        steps={tourSteps}
        onClose={() => setTourOpen(false)}
      />

      {/* WhatsApp-Style Dark Toast */}
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-5 left-1/2 z-50 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-[#111b21] px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white shadow-lg">
          {toast}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}


================================================================
FILE: src/components/DateDialModal.tsx
================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { DAY_NAMES_AR, MONTHS_AR, pad2, WEEK_ORDER_SAT_FRI } from '../data/seed';

interface DateDialModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  year: number;
  month1: number; // 1-12
  day: number; // 1-31
  onlyMonth?: boolean;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onConfirm: (result: { year: number; month1: number; day: number; iso: string }) => void;
}

const SIZE = 256;
const C = SIZE / 2;
const R = 98;

function daysIn(m1: number, y: number) {
  return new Date(y, m1, 0).getDate();
}

export const DateDialModal: React.FC<DateDialModalProps> = ({
  open,
  year,
  month1,
  day,
  onlyMonth = false,
  onCancel,
  onConfirm,
}) => {
  const [m, setM] = useState(month1);
  const [d, setD] = useState(day);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'month' | 'day'>('month');
  const [more, setMore] = useState(day > 15);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setM(month1);
    setD(day);
    setStep('month');
    setMore(day > 15);
  }, [open, month1, day]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const maxDay = daysIn(m, year);
  const lo = more ? 16 : 1;
  const hi = more ? maxDay : 15;
  const count = hi - lo + 1;

  const handAngle =
    step === 'month'
      ? ((m - 1) / 12) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2
      : ((d - lo) / count) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
      onClick={onCancel}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-[320px] rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xl"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h3 className="mb-3 text-center text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
          {step === 'month' ? 'اختر الشهر' : 'اختر اليوم'}
        </h3>

        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[#f0f2f5] p-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep('month')}
            className={`rounded-lg border px-3 py-1 text-sm font-bold transition-colors ${
              step === 'month'
                ? 'border-[#008069] bg-[#008069] text-white'
                : 'border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#54656f]'
            }`}
          >
            {MONTHS_AR[m - 1]}
          </button>
          {!onlyMonth && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setStep('day')}
              className={`font-mono-num rounded-lg border px-3 py-1 text-base font-bold transition-colors ${
                step === 'day'
                  ? 'border-[#008069] bg-[#008069] text-white'
                  : 'border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#54656f]'
              }`}
            >
              {d}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {step === 'day' && !onlyMonth && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              const nextMore = !more;
              setMore(nextMore);
              if (nextMore && d <= 15) setD(16);
              if (!nextMore && d > 15) setD(15);
            }}
            className="mb-3 w-full rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] hover:bg-[#d9fdd3]"
          >
            {more ? 'العودة للأيام (1 - 15)' : `أكثر (16 - ${maxDay})`}
          </button>
        )}

        <div
          dir="ltr"
          className="relative mx-auto mb-4 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc]"
          style={{ width: SIZE, height: SIZE }}
        >
          <div
            className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 h-0.5 origin-left bg-[#008069] transition-transform duration-150"
            style={{
              width: R,
              marginTop: -1,
              transform: `rotate(${handAngle}rad)`,
            }}
          />
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 h-2.5 w-2.5 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-[#008069]" />

          {step === 'month'
            ? MONTHS_AR.map((mName, idx) => {
                const mNum = idx + 1;
                const a = (idx / 12) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;
                const isSel = mNum === m;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={mNum}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setM(mNum);
                      setD((prev) => Math.min(prev, daysIn(mNum, year)));
                      if (!onlyMonth) {
                        setStep('day');
                        setMore(d > 15);
                      }
                    }}
                    style={{ left: C + R * Math.cos(a), top: C + R * Math.sin(a) }}
                    className={`absolute flex h-10 w-10 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-colors ${
                      isSel
                        ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                        : 'text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e9edef]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {mName}
                  </button>
                );
              })
            : Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => {
                const dayVal = lo + i;
                const a = (i / count) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;
                const isSel = dayVal === d;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={dayVal}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setD(dayVal)}
                    style={{ left: C + R * Math.cos(a), top: C + R * Math.sin(a) }}
                    className={`font-mono-num absolute flex h-8 w-8 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                      isSel
                        ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                        : 'text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e9edef]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {dayVal}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#3b4a54] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]"
          >
            إلغاء
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (step === 'month' && !onlyMonth) {
                setStep('day');
                setMore(d > 15);
                return;
              }
              onConfirm({
                year,
                month1: m,
                day: d,
                iso: `${year}-${pad2(m)}-${pad2(d)}`,
              });
            }}
            className="rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
          >
            {step === 'month' && !onlyMonth ? 'التالي لليوم' : 'تأكيد'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// CIRCULAR WEEKLY OFF DAY DIAL (السبت ← الجمعة + بدون)
// =========================================================================
interface WeeklyOffDialModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  selectedDay: number | null;
  title?: string;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onConfirm: (day: number | null) => void;
}

export const WeeklyOffDialModal: React.FC<WeeklyOffDialModalProps> = ({
  open,
  selectedDay,
  title = 'اختر يوم الإجازة الأسبوعية',
  onCancel,
  onConfirm,
}) => {
  const [val, setVal] = useState<number | null>(selectedDay);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) setVal(selectedDay);
  }, [open, selectedDay]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const selIdx = val === null ? -1 : WEEK_ORDER_SAT_FRI.indexOf(val as 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6);
  const handAngle = selIdx >= 0 ? (selIdx / 7) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2 : 0;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
      onClick={onCancel}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-[330px] rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xl"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h3 className="mb-1 text-center text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">{title}</h3>
        <p className="mb-4 text-center text-xs font-bold text-[#008069]">
          {val === null ? 'بدون إجازة أسبوعية' : `يوم الإجازة: ${DAY_NAMES_AR[val]}`}
        </p>

        <div
          dir="ltr"
          className="relative mx-auto mb-4 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc]"
          style={{ width: SIZE, height: SIZE }}
        >
          {selIdx >= 0 && (
            <div
              className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 h-0.5 origin-left bg-[#008069] transition-transform duration-150"
              style={{
                width: R - 8,
                marginTop: -1,
                transform: `rotate(${handAngle}rad)`,
              }}
            />
          )}

          {/* Center "بدون" button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setVal(null)}
            className={`absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 z-10 flex h-14 w-14 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full border text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
              val === null
                ? 'border-[#008069] bg-[#008069] text-white'
                : 'border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#54656f] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]'
            }`}
          >
            بدون
          </button>

          {WEEK_ORDER_SAT_FRI.map((dow, idx) => {
            const a = (idx / 7) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;
            const isSel = val === dow;
            return (
              <button
                key={dow}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setVal(dow)}
                style={{ left: C + R * Math.cos(a), top: C + R * Math.sin(a) }}
                className={`absolute flex h-12 w-12 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-extrabold shadow-xs transition-colors ${
                  isSel
                    ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white ring-2 ring-[#25d366]'
                    : 'border border-[#e9edef] bg-white text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]'
                }`}
              >
                {DAY_NAMES_AR[dow]}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#3b4a54] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]"
          >
            إلغاء
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onConfirm(val)}
            className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
          >
            تأكيد اليوم
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// =========================================================================
// CIRCULAR RECURRING MONTHLY LEAVE DIAL
// Step 1: Day of every month (e.g. day 5)
// Step 2: Number of days (e.g. 2 or 5 days)
// =========================================================================
interface MonthlyLeaveDialModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  initialDayOfMonth?: number;
  initialDaysCount?: number;
  initialSkipWeekly?: boolean;
  employeeName?: string;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onConfirm: (result: {
    dayOfMonth: number;
    daysCount: number;
    skipWeeklyInSameWeek: boolean;
  }) => void;
}

export const MonthlyLeaveDialModal: React.FC<MonthlyLeaveDialModalProps> = ({
  open,
  initialDayOfMonth = 5,
  initialDaysCount = 2,
  initialSkipWeekly = true,
  employeeName,
  onCancel,
  onConfirm,
}) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'day' | 'count'>('day');
  const [dayOfMonth, setDayOfMonth] = useState(initialDayOfMonth);
  const [moreDays, setMoreDays] = useState(initialDayOfMonth > 15);
  const [daysCount, setDaysCount] = useState(initialDaysCount);
  const [skipWeekly, setSkipWeekly] = useState(initialSkipWeekly);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const clampedDay = Math.min(31, Math.max(1, initialDayOfMonth || 5));
    const clampedCount = Math.min(15, Math.max(1, initialDaysCount || 2));
    setStep('day');
    setDayOfMonth(clampedDay);
    setMoreDays(clampedDay > 15);
    setDaysCount(clampedCount);
    setSkipWeekly(initialSkipWeekly);
  }, [open, initialDayOfMonth, initialDaysCount, initialSkipWeekly]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const lo = moreDays ? 16 : 1;
  const hi = moreDays ? 31 : 15;
  const dayOptionsCount = hi - lo + 1;
  const maxDurationOptions = 15;

  const handAngle =
    step === 'day'
      ? ((dayOfMonth - lo) / dayOptionsCount) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2
      : ((daysCount - 1) / maxDurationOptions) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
      onClick={onCancel}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-[340px] rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xl"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="mb-1 text-center text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
          {employeeName ? `إجازة شهرية — ${employeeName}` : 'الإجازة الشهرية المتكررة'}
        </div>
        <h3 className="mb-3 text-center text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
          {step === 'day' ? 'يأخذ إجازته يوم كام في كل شهر؟' : 'كم عدد أيام الإجازة؟'}
        </h3>

        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#f0f2f5] p-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep('day')}
            className={`flex-1 rounded-lg py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
              step === 'day'
                ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                : 'bg-white text-[#54656f]'
            }`}
          >
            يوم {dayOfMonth} كل شهر
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setStep('count')}
            className={`flex-1 rounded-lg py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
              step === 'count'
                ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                : 'bg-white text-[#54656f]'
            }`}
          >
            المدة: {daysCount} {daysCount === 1 ? 'يوم' : daysCount === 2 ? 'يومان' : 'أيام'}
          </button>
        </div>

        {step === 'day' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              const nextMore = !moreDays;
              setMoreDays(nextMore);
              if (nextMore && dayOfMonth <= 15) setDayOfMonth(16);
              if (!nextMore && dayOfMonth > 15) setDayOfMonth(15);
            }}
            className="mb-3 w-full rounded-full border border-dashed border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] hover:bg-[#d9fdd3]"
          >
            {moreDays ? 'العودة للأيام (1 - 15)' : 'الأيام (16 - 31)'}
          </button>
        )}

        <div
          dir="ltr"
          className="relative mx-auto mb-4 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc]"
          style={{ width: SIZE, height: SIZE }}
        >
          <div
            className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 h-0.5 origin-left bg-[#008069] transition-transform duration-150"
            style={{
              width: R,
              marginTop: -1,
              transform: `rotate(${handAngle}rad)`,
            }}
          />
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 flex h-12 w-12 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-sm font-extrabold text-white shadow-xs">
            {step === 'day' ? dayOfMonth : daysCount}
          </div>

          {step === 'day'
            ? Array.from({ length: dayOptionsCount }).map((_, i) => {
                const dayVal = lo + i;
                const a = (i / dayOptionsCount) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;
                const isSel = dayVal === dayOfMonth;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={dayVal}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDayOfMonth(dayVal)}
                    style={{ left: C + R * Math.cos(a), top: C + R * Math.sin(a) }}
                    className={`font-mono-num absolute flex h-8 w-8 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                      isSel
                        ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white ring-2 ring-[#25d366]'
                        : 'text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {dayVal}
                  </button>
                );
              })
            : Array.from({ length: maxDurationOptions }).map((_, i) => {
                const cntVal = i + 1;
                const a = (i / maxDurationOptions) * 2 * Math.PI - Math.PI / 2;
                const isSel = cntVal === daysCount;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={cntVal}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDaysCount(cntVal)}
                    style={{ left: C + R * Math.cos(a), top: C + R * Math.sin(a) }}
                    className={`font-mono-num absolute flex h-8 w-8 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                      isSel
                        ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white ring-2 ring-[#25d366]'
                        : 'text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {cntVal}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
        </div>

        {step === 'count' && (
          <label className="mb-3 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] p-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#3b4a54]">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={skipWeekly}
              onChange={(e) => setSkipWeekly(e.target.checked)}
            />
            <span>لا يأخذ إجازته الأسبوعية في نفس الأسبوع</span>
          </label>
        )}

        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {step === 'day' ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onCancel}
                className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#3b4a54]"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStep('count')}
                className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
              >
                التالي: عدد الأيام
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStep('day')}
                className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#3b4a54]"
              >
                رجوع لليوم
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  onConfirm({
                    dayOfMonth,
                    daysCount,
                    skipWeeklyInSameWeek: skipWeekly,
                  })
                }
                className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
              >
                حفظ الإجازة
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================
FILE: src/components/InteractiveTour.tsx
================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Compass, X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface TourStep {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  targetId?: string;
  fallbackTargetId?: string;
  actionLabel?: string;
  onEnter?: () => void;
  onNext?: () => void;
  isFinal?: boolean;
}

interface InteractiveTourProps {
  open: boolean;
  steps: TourStep[];
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const InteractiveTour: React.FC<InteractiveTourProps> = ({ open, steps, onClose }) => {
  const [stepIdx, setStepIdx] = useState(0);
  const stepsRef = useRef(steps);
  stepsRef.current = steps;
  const lastEnteredRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) {
      setStepIdx(0);
      lastEnteredRef.current = null;
      document.querySelectorAll('.tour-highlight').forEach((el) => {
        el.classList.remove('tour-highlight');
      });
      return;
    }

    const key = `${open}:${stepIdx}`;
    if (lastEnteredRef.current !== key) {
      lastEnteredRef.current = key;
      const step = stepsRef.current[stepIdx];
      if (step?.onEnter) {
        step.onEnter();
      }
    }
  }, [open, stepIdx]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const step = stepsRef.current[stepIdx];
    document.querySelectorAll('.tour-highlight').forEach((el) => {
      el.classList.remove('tour-highlight');
    });

    if (step?.targetId || step?.fallbackTargetId) {
      const ids = [step.targetId, step.fallbackTargetId].filter(Boolean) as string[];
      let attempts = 0;
      let timerId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

      const tryAttach = () => {
        const candidates = ids
          .map((id) => document.getElementById(id))
          .filter((el): el is HTMLElement => Boolean(el));
        const visibleEl =
          candidates.find((el) => el.offsetParent !== null) || candidates[0];

        if (visibleEl && visibleEl.offsetParent !== null) {
          visibleEl.classList.add('tour-highlight');
          visibleEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
          return;
        }
        attempts += 1;
        if (attempts < 10) {
          timerId = setTimeout(tryAttach, 80);
        }
      };

      timerId = setTimeout(tryAttach, 70);
      return () => {
        if (timerId) clearTimeout(timerId);
        document.querySelectorAll('.tour-highlight').forEach((el) => {
          el.classList.remove('tour-highlight');
        });
      };
    }
  }, [open, stepIdx]);

  if (!open || steps.length === 0) return null;
  const current = steps[stepIdx] || steps[0];
  const isLast = stepIdx === steps.length - 1;
  const progressPct = Math.round(((stepIdx + 1) / steps.length) * 100);

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (current.onNext) {
      current.onNext();
    }
    if (isLast) {
      onClose();
    } else {
      setStepIdx((i) => Math.min(steps.length - 1, i + 1));
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-3 z-50 flex justify-center px-3">
      <div className="pointer-events-auto w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#2a3942] bg-[#111b21] text-white shadow-2xl">
        {/* Progress bar */}
        <div className="h-1 w-full bg-[#202c33]">
          <div
            className="h-full bg-[#25d366] transition-all duration-300"
            style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 sm:p-5">
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-extrabold text-[#25d366]">
              <Compass className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>
                دليل أستاذ همام · {stepIdx + 1} من {steps.length}
              </span>
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="flex min-h-[32px] items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 text-[#aebac1] hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>إغلاق</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <h4 className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-extrabold text-white sm:text-base">
            {current.title}
          </h4>
          <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#d1d7db] sm:text-[13px]">
            {current.text}
          </p>

          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[#202c33] pt-3">
            {stepIdx > 0 ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStepIdx((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1))}
                className="min-h-[38px] rounded-full px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#aebac1] hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
              >
                رجوع
              </button>
            ) : (
              <span />
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleNext}
              className="flex min-h-[42px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#25d366] px-5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#1fc45b]"
            >
              <span>{isLast ? 'يلا نبدأ الشغل' : current.actionLabel || 'التالي'}</span>
              {!isLast && <ArrowLeft className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                >

                </button>

                  type="button"

                  className="flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58] sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <Compass className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>ابدأ الجولة العملية</span>
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIdx((prev) => prev + 1)}
                  className="flex min-h-[44px] items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-6 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58] sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <span>التالي</span>
                  <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/PlannerModule.tsx
================================================================
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays,
  Clock,
  Edit3,
  Moon,
  Plus,
  Search,
  Sun,
  Trash2,
  Users,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  computeDayPlanForBranch,
  DAY_NAMES_AR,
  formatTime12,
  getCairoToday,
  getRollingWeekQueue,
  pad2,
  SHIFTS_AR,
} from '../data/seed';
import {
  AppState,
  Branch,
  Employee,
  MonthlyLeave,
  ShiftType,
} from '../types';
import { MonthlyLeaveDialModal, WeeklyOffDialModal } from './DateDialModal';

export type PlannerTab = 'hub' | 'today' | 'team';

interface PlannerModuleProps {
  state: AppState;
  branchId: string;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  externalTab?: { tab: PlannerTab; nonce: number } | null;
  onSelectBranch: (bId: string) => void;
  onUpdateState: (updater: (prev: AppState) => AppState) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
  onGoToAvailability?: (shift: ShiftType) => void;
  onBackToAdminChats?: () => void;
}

export const PlannerModule: React.FC<PlannerModuleProps> = ({
  state,
  branchId,
  isAdmin,
  externalTab,
  onSelectBranch,
  onUpdateState,
  onNotify,
  onGoToAvailability,
  onBackToAdminChats,
}) => {
  const cairoToday = useMemo(() => getCairoToday(), []);
  const weekQueue = useMemo(() => getRollingWeekQueue(cairoToday.iso), [cairoToday.iso]);

  const [tab, setTab] = useState<PlannerTab>(isAdmin ? 'today' : 'hub');
  const [selectedIso, setSelectedIso] = useState<string>(cairoToday.iso);
  const [editModalShift, setEditModalShift] = useState<'morning' | 'evening' | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (externalTab) {
      setTab(isAdmin ? 'today' : externalTab.tab);
    }
  }, [externalTab?.nonce, externalTab?.tab, isAdmin]);

  // Add Employee form state (includes weekly off & recurring monthly leave)
  const [teamSearch, setTeamSearch] = useState('');
  const [newEmpName, setNewEmpName] = useState('');
  const [newEmpShift, setNewEmpShift] = useState<ShiftType>('morning');
  const [newEmpOffDay, setNewEmpOffDay] = useState<number | null>(5);
  const [newEmpMonthlyDraft, setNewEmpMonthlyDraft] = useState<{
    dayOfMonth: number;
    daysCount: number;
    skipWeeklyInSameWeek: boolean;
  } | null>(null);

  // Circular Weekly Off Dial state: 'new' for add-employee form, or empId for editing existing employee
  const [weeklyDialTarget, setWeeklyDialTarget] = useState<
    { mode: 'new' } | { mode: 'emp'; empId: string } | null
  >(null);

  // Circular Monthly Leave Dial state
  const [monthlyDialTarget, setMonthlyDialTarget] = useState<
    { mode: 'new' } | { mode: 'emp'; empId: string } | null
  >(null);

  // Manage existing employee's monthly leaves list modal (when they already have leaves)
  const [leaveListEmpId, setLeaveListEmpId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const branch: Branch =
    state.branches.find((b) => b.id === branchId) || state.branches[0];

  const activeDayObj = useMemo(() => {
    return weekQueue.find((d) => d.iso === selectedIso) || weekQueue[0];
  }, [weekQueue, selectedIso]);

  const activeDayPlan = useMemo(
    () => computeDayPlanForBranch(state, branch.id, activeDayObj.iso),
    [state, branch.id, activeDayObj.iso]
  );

  const dayGroups = useMemo(() => {
    const morningAvail = activeDayPlan.filter((p) => p.plannedShift === 'morning');
    const eveningAvail = activeDayPlan.filter((p) => p.plannedShift === 'evening');
    const betweenAvail = activeDayPlan.filter((p) => p.plannedShift === 'between');

    const morningOff = activeDayPlan.filter(
      (p) => p.plannedShift === 'off' && (p.emp.shift === 'morning' || p.emp.shift === 'between')
    );
    const eveningOff = activeDayPlan.filter(
      (p) => p.plannedShift === 'off' && (p.emp.shift === 'evening' || p.emp.shift === 'between')
    );
    const offAll = activeDayPlan.filter((p) => p.plannedShift === 'off');

    return {
      morningAvail,
      eveningAvail,
      betweenAvail,
      morningOff,
      eveningOff,
      totalWorking: morningAvail.length + eveningAvail.length + betweenAvail.length,
      totalOff: offAll.length,
    };
  }, [activeDayPlan]);

  const morningCfg = branch.shiftSchedules.morning;
  const eveningCfg = branch.shiftSchedules.evening;
  const betweenCfg = branch.shiftSchedules.between;

  const formatLeaveSummary = (ml: MonthlyLeave) => {
    const dLabel = ml.daysCount === 1 ? 'يوم' : ml.daysCount === 2 ? 'يومان' : `${ml.daysCount} أيام`;
    if (ml.recurringDayOfMonth) {
      return `يوم ${ml.recurringDayOfMonth} من كل شهر (${dLabel})`;
    }
    return `${ml.startDate} (${dLabel})`;
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // ADMIN READ-ONLY MODE: Side-by-Side Morning & Evening Roster (No Editing)
  // =========================================================================
  if (isAdmin) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-4 shadow-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {onBackToAdminChats && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onBackToAdminChats}
                className="flex min-h-[38px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e9edef]"
              >
                <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                <span>عودة للمحادثات</span>
              </button>
            )}
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
                مراجعة خطة الفرع (للقراءة فقط)
              </div>
              <h2 className="mt-0.5 text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-lg">
                فرع {branch.name} — {activeDayObj.arName} ({activeDayObj.shortDate})
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <select
            value={branch.id}
            onChange={(e) => onSelectBranch(e.target.value)}
            className="min-h-[42px] rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21] outline-none focus:border-[#008069]"
          >
            {state.branches.map((b) => (
              <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                فرع {b.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        {/* Rolling 7-Day Queue (1 horizontal row on mobile) */}
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-2.5 shadow-xs sm:p-3">
          <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-7 sm:pb-0">
            {weekQueue.map((wd) => {
              const isSel = wd.iso === activeDayObj.iso;
              const isToday = wd.offsetFromToday === 0;
              const isTomorrow = wd.offsetFromToday === 1;
              return (
                <button
                  key={wd.dayOfWeek}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedIso(wd.iso)}
                  className={`min-w-[82px] shrink-0 rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-2 text-right transition-colors sm:min-w-0 ${
                    isSel
                      ? 'border-[#008069] bg-[#008069] text-white'
                      : 'border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold sm:text-xs">
                    <span>{wd.arName}</span>
                    {isToday && (
                      <span
                        className={`rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] ${
                          isSel ? 'bg-white/20 text-white' : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                        }`}
                      >
                        اليوم
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {isTomorrow && (
                      <span
                        className={`rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] ${
                          isSel ? 'bg-[#25d366] text-[#111b21]' : 'bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#008069]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        غداً
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div
                    className={`font-mono-num mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold sm:text-[11px] ${
                      isSel ? 'text-white/85' : 'text-[#54656f]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {wd.shortDate}
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Two Horizontal Side-by-Side Read-Only Cards Even on Mobile */}
        <div id="plannerDaySummary" className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-4">
          <div className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-2.5 shadow-xs sm:p-5">
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1.5 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-2 sm:mb-3 sm:pb-3">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2.5">
                <div className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 sm:h-9 sm:w-9">
                  <Sun className="h-4 w-4 sm:h-5 sm:w-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h3 className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-base">
                    صباحي
                  </h3>
                  <div className="font-mono-num truncate text-[10px] text-[#54656f] sm:text-xs">
                    {formatTime12(morningCfg.tin)} — {formatTime12(morningCfg.tout)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-3 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                {dayGroups.morningAvail.length + dayGroups.betweenAvail.length} متاح
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
              {dayGroups.morningAvail.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={p.emp.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#111b21] sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2.5 sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-[#008069] sm:text-xs">حضور</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {dayGroups.betweenAvail.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={`${p.emp.id}_rm`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#111b21] sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2.5 sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-amber-800 sm:text-xs">
                    بتوين {formatTime12(betweenCfg.tin)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {dayGroups.morningOff.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={`${p.emp.id}_om`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/70 px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-900 sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2 sm:text-xs"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px]">{p.reason}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-2.5 shadow-xs sm:p-5">
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1.5 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-2 sm:mb-3 sm:pb-3">
              <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2.5">
                <div className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-white sm:h-9 sm:w-9">
                  <Moon className="h-4 w-4 sm:h-5 sm:w-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h3 className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-base">
                    مسائي
                  </h3>
                  <div className="font-mono-num truncate text-[10px] text-[#54656f] sm:text-xs">
                    {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tin)} — {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tout)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-3 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                {dayGroups.eveningAvail.length + dayGroups.betweenAvail.length} متاح
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
              {dayGroups.eveningAvail.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={p.emp.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#111b21] sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2.5 sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-[#008069] sm:text-xs">حضور</span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {dayGroups.betweenAvail.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={`${p.emp.id}_re`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#111b21] sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2.5 sm:text-sm"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-amber-800 sm:text-xs">
                    بتوين حتى {formatTime12(betweenCfg.tout)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {dayGroups.eveningOff.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={`${p.emp.id}_oe`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/70 px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-900 sm:px-3.5 sm:py-2 sm:text-xs"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{p.emp.name}</span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px]">{p.reason}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // BRANCH MANAGER PLANNER HELPERS
  // =========================================================================
  const setDailyOverride = (
    empId: string,
    patch: { shift?: ShiftType | 'off' } | null
  ) => {
    const key = `${branch.id}|${activeDayObj.iso}`;
    onUpdateState((prev) => {
      const nextOverrides = { ...prev.dailyOverrides };
      const dayMap = { ...(nextOverrides[key] || {}) };
      if (patch === null) {
        delete dayMap[empId];
      } else {
        dayMap[empId] = { ...(dayMap[empId] || {}), ...patch };
      }
      nextOverrides[key] = dayMap;
      return { ...prev, dailyOverrides: nextOverrides };
    });
  };

  const updateEmployee = (empId: string, patch: Partial<Employee>) => {
    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
        b.id !== branch.id
          ? b
          : {
              ...b,
              emps: b.emps.map((e) => (e.id === empId ? { ...e, ...patch } : e)),
            }
      ),
    }));
  };

  const handleAddEmployee = () => {
    const name = newEmpName.trim();
    if (!name) {
      onNotify('يرجى كتابة اسم الموظف');
      return;
    }

    const monthlyLeaves: MonthlyLeave[] = [];
    if (newEmpMonthlyDraft) {
      const refMonth = pad2(cairoToday.month + 1);
      const refDay = pad2(newEmpMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth);
      monthlyLeaves.push({
        id: `ml_${Date.now()}`,
        recurringDayOfMonth: newEmpMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth,
        startDate: `${cairoToday.year}-${refMonth}-${refDay}`,
        daysCount: newEmpMonthlyDraft.daysCount,
        skipWeeklyInSameWeek: newEmpMonthlyDraft.skipWeeklyInSameWeek,
      });
    }

    const newEmp: Employee = {
      id: `e_${branch.id}_${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      shift: newEmpShift,
      suspended: false,
      weeklyOffDay: newEmpOffDay,
      extraOffDay1: null,
      extraOffDay2: null,
      monthlyLeaves,
    };

    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
        b.id === branch.id ? { ...b, emps: [...b.emps, newEmp] } : b
      ),
    }));
    setNewEmpName('');
    setNewEmpMonthlyDraft(null);
    onNotify(`تمت إضافة ${name} بنجاح`);
  };

  const handleDeleteEmployee = (emp: Employee) => {
    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
        b.id === branch.id
          ? { ...b, emps: b.emps.filter((e) => e.id !== emp.id) }
          : b
      ),
    }));
    onNotify(`تم حذف ${emp.name}`);
  };

  const leaveListEmp = branch.emps.find((e) => e.id === leaveListEmpId) || null;
  const weeklyDialEmp =
    weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'emp'
      ? branch.emps.find((e) => e.id === weeklyDialTarget.empId) || null
      : null;
  const monthlyDialEmp =
    monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'emp'
      ? branch.emps.find((e) => e.id === monthlyDialTarget.empId) || null
      : null;

  const filteredTeam = branch.emps.filter(
    (e) => !e.suspended && e.name.toLowerCase().includes(teamSearch.trim().toLowerCase())
  );

  const teamByShift = {
    morning: filteredTeam.filter((e) => e.shift === 'morning'),
    between: filteredTeam.filter((e) => e.shift === 'between'),
    evening: filteredTeam.filter((e) => e.shift === 'evening'),
  };

  const editModalItems = !editModalShift
    ? []
    : activeDayPlan.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.plannedShift === editModalShift ||
          p.plannedShift === 'between' ||
          (p.plannedShift === 'off' &&
            (p.emp.shift === editModalShift || p.emp.shift === 'between'))
      );

  // =========================================================================
  // CENTERED 2-OPTION HUB (لوحة اليوم والجدول | الموظفون والإجازات)
