  // =========================================================================
  if (tab === 'hub') {
    const hubItems = [
      {
        id: 'today' as PlannerTab,
        title: 'لوحة اليوم والجدول',
        desc: 'عرض الصباحي والمسائي لأي يوم من السبت للجمعة وتعديل الحضور',
        icon: CalendarDays,
        badge: `${dayGroups.totalWorking} حاضر اليوم`,
        color: 'bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#008069]',
      },
      {
        id: 'team' as PlannerTab,
        title: 'الموظفون والإجازات',
        desc: 'إضافة موظف مع إجازته الأسبوعية والشهرية عبر الاختيار الدائري',
        icon: Users,
        badge: `${branch.emps.filter((e) => !e.suspended).length} موظف`,
        color: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
      },
    ];

    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-[65vh] items-center justify-center px-2 py-4">
        <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-6 text-center">
            <div className="text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
              خطة فرع {branch.name}
            </div>
            <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
              اختر القسم الذي تريد فتحه
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#54656f]">
              مواعيد الورديات ثابتة: الصباحي {formatTime12(morningCfg.tin)} —{' '}
              {formatTime12(morningCfg.tout)} · المسائي {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tin)} —{' '}
              {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tout)}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {hubItems.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  id={`plannerTab_${item.id}`}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTab(item.id)}
                  className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] p-5 text-right transition-all hover:border-[#008069] hover:bg-[#f0f9f4]"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div
                        className={`flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${item.color}`}
                      >
                        <Icon className="h-6 w-6" />
                      </div>
                      <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#54656f] shadow-xs">
                        {item.badge}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                      {item.title}
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#54656f]">
                      {item.desc}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Sub-Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-4 shadow-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab('hub')}
            className="flex min-h-[40px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e9edef]"
          >
            <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
            <span>القائمة الرئيسية</span>
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
            فرع {branch.name}
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#f0f2f5] p-1">
          {[
            { id: 'today', label: 'لوحة اليوم والجدول' },
            { id: 'team', label: 'الموظفون والإجازات' },
          ].map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              id={`plannerTab_${t.id}`}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTab(t.id as PlannerTab)}
              className={`whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                tab === t.id
                  ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                  : 'text-[#54656f] hover:text-[#111b21]'
              }`}
            >
              {t.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TAB 1: لوحة اليوم (أفقية على الهاتف مع طابور الأيام السبعة) */}
      {tab === 'today' && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-2.5 shadow-xs sm:p-3.5">
            <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 sm:grid sm:grid-cols-7 sm:pb-0">
              {weekQueue.map((wd) => {
                const isSel = wd.iso === activeDayObj.iso;
                const isToday = wd.offsetFromToday === 0;
                const isTomorrow = wd.offsetFromToday === 1;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={wd.dayOfWeek}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedIso(wd.iso)}
                    className={`min-w-[82px] shrink-0 rounded-xl border px-2.5 py-2 text-right transition-colors sm:min-w-0 sm:px-3 sm:py-2.5 ${
                      isSel
                        ? 'border-[#008069] bg-[#008069] text-white'
                        : 'border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold sm:text-xs">
                      <span>{wd.arName}</span>
                      {isToday && (
                        <span
                          className={`rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] ${
                            isSel ? 'bg-white/20 text-white' : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                          }`}
                        >
                          اليوم
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {isTomorrow && (
                        <span
                          className={`rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] ${
                            isSel ? 'bg-[#25d366] text-[#111b21]' : 'bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#008069]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          غداً
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div
                      className={`font-mono-num mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold sm:mt-1 sm:text-xs ${
                        isSel ? 'text-white/85' : 'text-[#54656f]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {wd.shortDate}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            id="plannerDaySummary"
            className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-3 shadow-xs sm:p-5"
          >
            <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-3 sm:mb-5 sm:pb-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-lg">
                  توزيع ورديات يوم {activeDayObj.arName} · {activeDayObj.shortDate}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#54656f] sm:text-xs">
                  الحضور: {dayGroups.totalWorking} · الإجازات اليوم: {dayGroups.totalOff}
                </p>
              </div>

              {onGoToAvailability && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onGoToAvailability('morning')}
                  className="min-h-[38px] rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58] sm:min-h-[42px] sm:px-5 sm:py-2"
                >
                  إرسال توفر الغد في المحادثة
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Horizontal Side-by-Side Morning & Evening on Mobile (grid-cols-2) */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:gap-5">
              {/* Morning Card */}
              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] p-2.5 sm:p-5">
                <div>
                  <div className="mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1.5 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-2 sm:mb-4 sm:pb-3">
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2.5">
                      <div className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 sm:h-9 sm:w-9">
                        <Sun className="h-4 w-4 sm:h-5 sm:w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h4 className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-base">
                          صباحي
                        </h4>
                        <div className="font-mono-num truncate text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#54656f] sm:text-xs">
                          {formatTime12(morningCfg.tin)} — {formatTime12(morningCfg.tout)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-3 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                      {dayGroups.morningAvail.length} متاح
                      {dayGroups.betweenAvail.length > 0
                        ? ` + ${dayGroups.betweenAvail.length} بتوين`
                        : ''}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
                    {dayGroups.morningAvail.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={p.emp.id}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-white px-2 py-2 sm:px-4 sm:py-3"
                      >
                        <span className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                          {p.emp.name}
                        </span>
                        <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                          متاح
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    {dayGroups.betweenAvail.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={`${p.emp.id}_bm`}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/60 px-2 py-2 sm:px-4 sm:py-3"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                            {p.emp.name}
                          </div>
                          <div className="truncate text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 sm:text-[11px]">
                            بتوين · {formatTime12(betweenCfg.tin)}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                          متاح
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  {dayGroups.morningOff.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-3 space-y-1.5 sm:mt-4 sm:space-y-2">
                      <div className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-rose-700 sm:text-xs">
                        غير المتاحين:
                      </div>
                      {dayGroups.morningOff.map((p) => (
                        <div
                          key={`${p.emp.id}_om`}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/70 px-2 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2.5"
                        >
                          <span className="truncate text-xs font-bold text-rose-950 sm:text-sm">
                            {p.emp.name}
                          </span>
                          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-rose-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-800 sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                            {p.reason}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEditModalShift('morning')}
                  className="mt-3 flex min-h-[38px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5] sm:mt-5 sm:min-h-[42px] sm:gap-1.5 sm:py-2 sm:text-xs"
                >
                  <Edit3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#008069]" />
                  <span>تعديل الصباحي</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Evening Card */}
              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] p-2.5 sm:p-5">
                <div>
                  <div className="mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1.5 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-2 sm:mb-4 sm:pb-3">
                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2.5">
                      <div className="flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-white sm:h-9 sm:w-9">
                        <Moon className="h-4 w-4 sm:h-5 sm:w-5" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h4 className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-base">
                          مسائي
                        </h4>
                        <div className="font-mono-num truncate text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#54656f] sm:text-xs">
                          {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tin)} — {formatTime12(eveningCfg.tout)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <span className="rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-3 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                      {dayGroups.eveningAvail.length} متاح
                      {dayGroups.betweenAvail.length > 0
                        ? ` + ${dayGroups.betweenAvail.length} بتوين`
                        : ''}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:space-y-2">
                    {dayGroups.eveningAvail.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={p.emp.id}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-white px-2 py-2 sm:px-4 sm:py-3"
                      >
                        <span className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                          {p.emp.name}
                        </span>
                        <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                          متاح
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    {dayGroups.betweenAvail.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={`${p.emp.id}_be`}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/60 px-2 py-2 sm:px-4 sm:py-3"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="truncate text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                            {p.emp.name}
                          </div>
                          <div className="truncate text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 sm:text-[11px]">
                            بتوين · حتى {formatTime12(betweenCfg.tout)}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#006e5a] sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                          متاح
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  {dayGroups.eveningOff.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-3 space-y-1.5 sm:mt-4 sm:space-y-2">
                      <div className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-rose-700 sm:text-xs">
                        غير المتاحين:
                      </div>
                      {dayGroups.eveningOff.map((p) => (
                        <div
                          key={`${p.emp.id}_oe`}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/70 px-2 py-1.5 sm:px-4 sm:py-2.5"
                        >
                          <span className="truncate text-xs font-bold text-rose-950 sm:text-sm">
                            {p.emp.name}
                          </span>
                          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-rose-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-rose-800 sm:px-2.5 sm:py-1 sm:text-xs">
                            {p.reason}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEditModalShift('evening')}
                  className="mt-3 flex min-h-[38px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-2 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5] sm:mt-5 sm:min-h-[42px] sm:gap-1.5 sm:py-2 sm:text-xs"
                >
                  <Edit3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#008069]" />
                  <span>تعديل المسائي</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TAB 2: الموظفون والإجازات (مع الاختيار الدائري) */}
      {tab === 'team' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xs">
            <h3 className="mb-1 text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
              إضافة موظف جديد مع إجازته
            </h3>
            <p className="mb-4 text-xs text-[#54656f]">
              اكتب الاسم، اختر الشفت، وحدد الإجازة الأسبوعية والشهرية بالساعة الدائرية
            </p>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                    اسم الموظف
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="مثال: أحمد طارق"
                    value={newEmpName}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewEmpName(e.target.value)}
                    className="min-h-[44px] w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-bold outline-none focus:border-[#008069]"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                    الشفت
                  </label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
                    {(['morning', 'between', 'evening'] as ShiftType[]).map((sh) => (
                      <button
                        key={sh}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setNewEmpShift(sh)}
                        className={`min-h-[44px] rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                          newEmpShift === sh
                            ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                            : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] text-[#3b4a54] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {SHIFTS_AR[sh]}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Circular Pickers Row for Weekly Off & Monthly Leave */}
              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setWeeklyDialTarget({ mode: 'new' })}
                  className="flex min-h-[48px] items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] px-4 py-2.5 text-right transition-colors hover:border-[#008069] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]/50"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Clock className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                      الإجازة الأسبوعية (دائري):
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-white">
                    {newEmpOffDay === null ? 'بدون' : DAY_NAMES_AR[newEmpOffDay]}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setMonthlyDialTarget({ mode: 'new' })}
                    className="flex min-h-[48px] flex-1 items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] px-4 py-2.5 text-right transition-colors hover:border-[#008069] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]/50"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                        إجازة شهرية (دائري):
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                        newEmpMonthlyDraft
                          ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                          : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#54656f]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {newEmpMonthlyDraft
                        ? `يوم ${newEmpMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth} (${newEmpMonthlyDraft.daysCount} يوم)`
                        : 'تحديد بالدائرية'}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                  {newEmpMonthlyDraft && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setNewEmpMonthlyDraft(null)}
                      aria-label="مسح الإجازة الشهرية"
                      className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100"
                    >
                      <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleAddEmployee}
                className="flex min-h-[46px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2.5 text-sm font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
              >
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>إضافة للطاقم</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xs">
            <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-4">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                  تنظيم الموظفين والإجازات ({filteredTeam.length})
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                  اختر الشفت أو اضغط على الإجازة للتعديل بالساعة الدائرية
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
                <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#667781]" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="بحث بالاسم..."
                  value={teamSearch}
                  onChange={(e) => setTeamSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-full bg-[#f0f2f5] py-2 pl-3 pr-9 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#008069]"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-6">
              {[
                {
                  sh: 'morning' as ShiftType,
                  title: 'الوردية الصباحية',
                  hint: `${formatTime12(morningCfg.tin)} — ${formatTime12(morningCfg.tout)}`,
                  items: teamByShift.morning,
                  accent: 'border-[#86efac] bg-[#f4fbf6]',
                },
                {
                  sh: 'between' as ShiftType,
                  title: 'موظفو بتوين',
                  hint: `يظهرون ضمن الصباحي والمسائي (${formatTime12(betweenCfg.tin)} — ${formatTime12(betweenCfg.tout)})`,
                  items: teamByShift.between,
                  accent: 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50',
                },
                {
                  sh: 'evening' as ShiftType,
                  title: 'الوردية المسائية',
                  hint: `${formatTime12(eveningCfg.tin)} — ${formatTime12(eveningCfg.tout)}`,
                  items: teamByShift.evening,
                  accent: 'border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc]',
                },
              ].map((sec) => (
                <section key={sec.sh}>
                  <div className="mb-3">
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                      {sec.title} ({sec.items.length})
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">{sec.hint}</p>
                  </div>

                  {sec.items.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[#d1d7db] py-4 text-center text-xs text-[#667781]">
                      لا يوجد موظفون في هذا القسم
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
                      {sec.items.map((emp) => (
                        <div
                          key={emp.id}
                          className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 transition-colors ${sec.accent}`}
                        >
                          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                              <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069] shadow-xs">
                                {emp.name.slice(0, 1)}
                              </div>
                              <div className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                                {emp.name}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleDeleteEmployee(emp)}
                              aria-label={`حذف ${emp.name}`}
                              className="rounded-full border border-rose-200 bg-white p-1.5 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50"
                            >
                              <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                            </button>
                          </div>

                          <div className="space-y-2.5">
                            <div>
                              <div className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                                الشفت
                              </div>
                              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
                                {(['morning', 'between', 'evening'] as ShiftType[]).map((sh) => (
                                  <button
                                    key={sh}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => updateEmployee(emp.id, { shift: sh })}
                                    className={`min-h-[36px] rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold ${
                                      emp.shift === sh
                                        ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                                        : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#3b4a54] hover:bg-[#f0f2f5]'
                                    }`}
                                  >
                                    {SHIFTS_AR[sh]}
                                  </button>
                                ))}
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-1">
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  setWeeklyDialTarget({ mode: 'emp', empId: emp.id })
                                }
                                className="flex min-h-[40px] items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:border-[#008069]"
                              >
                                <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#008069]" />
                                <span>
                                  {emp.weeklyOffDay === null
                                    ? 'أسبوعية: بدون'
                                    : `أسبوعية: ${DAY_NAMES_AR[emp.weeklyOffDay]}`}
                                </span>
                              </button>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                  if ((emp.monthlyLeaves || []).length === 0) {
                                    setMonthlyDialTarget({ mode: 'emp', empId: emp.id });
                                  } else {
                                    setLeaveListEmpId(emp.id);
                                  }
                                }}
                                className="flex min-h-[40px] items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#008069] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]"
                              >
                                <CalendarDays className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                <span>
                                  {(emp.monthlyLeaves || []).length > 0
                                    ? `شهرية (${emp.monthlyLeaves.length})`
                                    : 'إجازة شهرية'}
                                </span>
                              </button>
                            </div>

                            {(emp.monthlyLeaves || []).length > 0 && (
                              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
                                {emp.monthlyLeaves.map((ml) => (
                                  <span
                                    key={ml.id}
                                    className="rounded-full bg-white px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#006e5a] shadow-xs"
                                  >
                                    {formatLeaveSummary(ml)}
                                  </span>
                                ))}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal: تعديل أفراد الصباحي أو المسائي لهذا اليوم */}
      {editModalShift && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
          onClick={() => setEditModalShift(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-3">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                  تعديل أفراد {editModalShift === 'morning' ? 'الصباحي' : 'المسائي'} —{' '}
                  {activeDayObj.arName} ({activeDayObj.shortDate})
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                  اضغط على الشفت أو الإجازة لهذا اليوم فقط
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEditModalShift(null)}
                className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]"
              >
                إغلاق
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {editModalItems.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={p.emp.id}
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] px-4 py-3"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">{p.emp.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                      الأساسي: {SHIFTS_AR[p.emp.shift]}
                      {p.isOverridden ? ' · معدل لهذا اليوم' : ''}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                    {(
                      [
                        { val: 'morning', label: 'صباحي' },
                        { val: 'between', label: 'بتوين' },
                        { val: 'evening', label: 'مسائي' },
                        { val: 'off', label: 'إجازة' },
                      ] as const
                    ).map((opt) => (
                      <button
                        key={opt.val}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setDailyOverride(p.emp.id, { shift: opt.val })
                        }
                        className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                          p.plannedShift === opt.val
                            ? opt.val === 'off'
                              ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                              : 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                            : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#3b4a54]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {opt.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                    {p.isOverridden && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setDailyOverride(p.emp.id, null)}
                        className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]"
                      >
                        استعادة
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal: قائمة الإجازات الشهرية للموظف مع زر إضافة بالدائرية */}
      {leaveListEmp && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
          onClick={() => setLeaveListEmpId(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-3">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                  الإجازة الشهرية — {leaveListEmp.name}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">
                  يتكرر تلقائياً في الموعد المحدد كل شهر
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setLeaveListEmpId(null)}
                className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold"
              >
                إغلاق
              </button>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                const targetId = leaveListEmp.id;
                setLeaveListEmpId(null);
                setMonthlyDialTarget({ mode: 'emp', empId: targetId });
              }}
              className="mb-4 flex min-h-[44px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2.5 text-sm font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>إضافة إجازة شهرية بالدائرية</span>
            </button>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              {(leaveListEmp.monthlyLeaves || []).map((ml) => (
                <div
                  key={ml.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                      {formatLeaveSummary(ml)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-[#54656f]">
                      {ml.skipWeeklyInSameWeek
                        ? 'بدون إجازة أسبوعية في نفس الأسبوع'
                        : 'مع احتساب الإجازة الأسبوعية'}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      updateEmployee(leaveListEmp.id, {
                        monthlyLeaves: (leaveListEmp.monthlyLeaves || []).filter(
                          (x) => x.id !== ml.id
                        ),
                      });
                      onNotify('تم حذف الإجازة الشهرية');
                    }}
                    className="rounded-full border border-rose-200 bg-white px-3 py-1 font-bold text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50"
                  >
                    حذف
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Circular Weekly Off Day Dial */}
      <WeeklyOffDialModal
        open={weeklyDialTarget !== null}
        selectedDay={
          weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newEmpOffDay
            : weeklyDialEmp?.weeklyOffDay ?? null
        }
        title={
          weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? 'اختر يوم الإجازة الأسبوعية'
            : weeklyDialEmp
            ? `الإجازة الأسبوعية — ${weeklyDialEmp.name}`
            : 'اختر يوم الإجازة الأسبوعية'
        }
        onCancel={() => setWeeklyDialTarget(null)}
        onConfirm={(pickedDay) => {
          if (weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'new') {
            setNewEmpOffDay(pickedDay);
          } else if (weeklyDialEmp) {
            updateEmployee(weeklyDialEmp.id, { weeklyOffDay: pickedDay });
            onNotify(
              pickedDay === null
                ? `تم إلغاء الإجازة الأسبوعية لـ ${weeklyDialEmp.name}`
                : `تم تحديد إجازة ${weeklyDialEmp.name}: ${DAY_NAMES_AR[pickedDay]}`
            );
          }
          setWeeklyDialTarget(null);
        }}
      />

      {/* Circular Recurring Monthly Leave Dial */}
      <MonthlyLeaveDialModal
        open={monthlyDialTarget !== null}
        initialDayOfMonth={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newEmpMonthlyDraft?.dayOfMonth ?? 5
            : 5
        }
        initialDaysCount={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newEmpMonthlyDraft?.daysCount ?? 2
            : 2
        }
        initialSkipWeekly={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newEmpMonthlyDraft?.skipWeeklyInSameWeek ?? true
            : true
        }
        employeeName={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newEmpName.trim() || 'موظف جديد'
            : monthlyDialEmp?.name
        }
        onCancel={() => setMonthlyDialTarget(null)}
        onConfirm={(res) => {
          if (monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new') {
            setNewEmpMonthlyDraft(res);
          } else if (monthlyDialEmp) {
            const refMonth = pad2(cairoToday.month + 1);
            const refDay = pad2(res.dayOfMonth);
            const newLeave: MonthlyLeave = {
              id: `ml_${Date.now()}`,
              recurringDayOfMonth: res.dayOfMonth,
              startDate: `${cairoToday.year}-${refMonth}-${refDay}`,
              daysCount: res.daysCount,
              skipWeeklyInSameWeek: res.skipWeeklyInSameWeek,
            };
            updateEmployee(monthlyDialEmp.id, {
              monthlyLeaves: [...(monthlyDialEmp.monthlyLeaves || []), newLeave],
            });
            onNotify(
              `تم حفظ إجازة ${monthlyDialEmp.name}: يوم ${res.dayOfMonth} كل شهر (${res.daysCount} يوم)`
            );
          }
          setMonthlyDialTarget(null);
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================
FILE: src/components/BranchAvailabilityView.tsx
================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowRight,
  Building2,
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  CheckCheck,
  Clock,
  Edit3,
  Moon,
  Plus,
  Send,
  Sparkles,
  Sun,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  addDaysIso,
  buildPlannedRowsForShift,
  computeDayPlanForBranch,
  DAY_NAMES_AR,
  evaluateAvailabilityCompliance,
  fmtDateAr,
  formatAvailabilityChatText,
  getCairoToday,
  pad2,
  SHIFT_FULL_AR,
  SHIFT_START_DESC,
  SHIFTS_AR,
  submissionKey,
  UNAVAILABLE_REASONS,
} from '../data/seed';
import {
  AppState,
  AvailabilityRow,
  Branch,
  ChatMessage,
  Employee,
  MonthlyLeave,
  SessionState,
  ShiftType,
} from '../types';
import { MonthlyLeaveDialModal, WeeklyOffDialModal } from './DateDialModal';

interface BranchAvailabilityViewProps {
  state: AppState;
  session: SessionState;
  branch: Branch;
  focusEvent?: {
    branchId: string;
    shift: ShiftType;
    messageId?: string;
    nonce: number;
  } | null;
  onChangeSession: (patch: Partial<SessionState>) => void;
  onUpdateState: (updater: (prev: AppState) => AppState) => void;
  onNotify: (msg: string) => void;
  onOpenPlanner: () => void;
}

export const BranchAvailabilityView: React.FC<BranchAvailabilityViewProps> = ({
  state,
  session,
  branch,
  focusEvent,
  onChangeSession,
  onUpdateState,
  onNotify,
  onOpenPlanner,
}) => {
  const cairoToday = useMemo(() => getCairoToday(), []);
  const tomorrowIso = useMemo(() => addDaysIso(cairoToday.iso, 1), [cairoToday.iso]);
  const { branchScreen, activeShift } = session;

  const [draftRows, setDraftRows] = useState<AvailabilityRow[]>([]);
  const [confirmModalOpen, setConfirmModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState('');
  const [highlightedMsgId, setHighlightedMsgId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const chatEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const msgRefs = useRef<Record<string, HTMLDivElement | null>>({});

  // Emergency modal
  const [emergencyOpen, setEmergencyOpen] = useState(false);
  const [emergencyEmpId, setEmergencyEmpId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [emergencyReason, setEmergencyReason] = useState<string>('');

  // Add employee in Branch Staff screen (with circular weekly & monthly dials)
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newShift, setNewShift] = useState<ShiftType>('morning');
  const [newOffDay, setNewOffDay] = useState<number | null>(5);
  const [newMonthlyDraft, setNewMonthlyDraft] = useState<{
    dayOfMonth: number;
    daysCount: number;
    skipWeeklyInSameWeek: boolean;
  } | null>(null);

  const [weeklyDialTarget, setWeeklyDialTarget] = useState<
    { mode: 'new' } | { mode: 'emp'; empId: string } | null
  >(null);
  const [monthlyDialTarget, setMonthlyDialTarget] = useState<
    { mode: 'new' } | { mode: 'emp'; empId: string } | null
  >(null);

  const tomorrowPlan = useMemo(
    () => computeDayPlanForBranch(state, branch.id, tomorrowIso),
    [state, branch.id, tomorrowIso]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (branchScreen !== 'submit') return;
    const key = submissionKey(branch.id, activeShift, tomorrowIso);
    const existing = state.submissions[key];
    const includesBetween = activeShift === 'morning' || activeShift === 'evening';
    const shiftEmps = tomorrowPlan.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.plannedShift === activeShift ||
        (includesBetween && p.plannedShift === 'between') ||
        (p.plannedShift === 'off' &&
          (p.emp.shift === activeShift || (includesBetween && p.emp.shift === 'between')))
    );

    if (existing) {
      const existingMap = new Map(existing.rows.map((r) => [r.empId, r]));
      const merged: AvailabilityRow[] = shiftEmps.map((p) => {
        const prev = existingMap.get(p.emp.id);
        if (prev) return { ...prev };
        return {
          empId: p.emp.id,
          empName: p.emp.name,
          status: 'available',
          reason: null,
        };
      });
      setDraftRows(merged);
    } else {
      setDraftRows(
        shiftEmps.map((p) => ({
          empId: p.emp.id,
          empName: p.emp.name,
          status: 'available',
          reason: null,
        }))
      );
    }
  }, [branchScreen, activeShift, branch.id, tomorrowIso, state.submissions, tomorrowPlan]);

  const branchMessages = useMemo(
    () => state.messages.filter((m) => m.branchId === branch.id),
    [state.messages, branch.id]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (branchScreen !== 'home') return;
    if (focusEvent && focusEvent.branchId === branch.id) {
      const targetId =
        focusEvent.messageId ||
        [...branchMessages]
          .reverse()
          .find((m) => m.shift === focusEvent.shift || m.sender === 'admin')?.id;
      if (targetId && msgRefs.current[targetId]) {
        setHighlightedMsgId(targetId);
        setTimeout(() => {
          msgRefs.current[targetId]?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        }, 60);
        const timer = setTimeout(() => setHighlightedMsgId(null), 3500);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }
    chatEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [branchScreen, branchMessages.length, focusEvent?.nonce]);

  const morningSub = state.submissions[submissionKey(branch.id, 'morning', tomorrowIso)];
  const eveningSub = state.submissions[submissionKey(branch.id, 'evening', tomorrowIso)];

  const submittedForTomorrow = useMemo(() => {
    const list: Array<{ shift: ShiftType; rows: AvailabilityRow[] }> = [];
    (['morning', 'evening'] as ShiftType[]).forEach((sh) => {
      const sub = state.submissions[submissionKey(branch.id, sh, tomorrowIso)];
      if (sub) list.push({ shift: sh, rows: sub.rows });
    });
    return list;
  }, [state.submissions, branch.id, tomorrowIso]);

  const liveCompliance = useMemo(
    () =>
      evaluateAvailabilityCompliance(
        state,
        branch.id,
        activeShift,
        tomorrowIso,
        draftRows
      ),
    [state, branch.id, activeShift, tomorrowIso, draftRows]
  );

  const availCount = draftRows.filter((r) => r.status === 'available').length;
  const unavailCount = draftRows.filter((r) => r.status === 'unavailable').length;

  const dispatchAvailabilitySubmission = (shift: ShiftType, rowsToSend: AvailabilityRow[]) => {
    const key = submissionKey(branch.id, shift, tomorrowIso);
    const prevSub = state.submissions[key];
    const compliance = evaluateAvailabilityCompliance(
      state,
      branch.id,
      shift,
      tomorrowIso,
      rowsToSend
    );
    const isMatched = compliance.status === 'matched';
    const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });

    const isEditedResend = Boolean(prevSub);
    const editChanges: string[] = [];
    if (prevSub) {
      const prevMap = new Map<string, AvailabilityRow>(
        prevSub.rows.map((r) => [r.empId, r])
      );
      rowsToSend.forEach((r) => {
        const old = prevMap.get(r.empId);
        if (!old) {
          editChanges.push(
            `إضافة ${r.empName} (${r.status === 'available' ? 'حضور' : r.reason || 'غير متاح'})`
          );
        } else if (old.status !== r.status || old.reason !== r.reason) {
          if (r.status === 'unavailable') {
            editChanges.push(`${r.empName}: تغيّر إلى غير متاح (${r.reason || 'بدون سبب'})`);
          } else {
            editChanges.push(`${r.empName}: تغيّر إلى حضور`);
          }
        }
      });
      prevSub.rows.forEach((old) => {
        if (!rowsToSend.some((r) => r.empId === old.empId)) {
          editChanges.push(`حذف ${old.empName}`);
        }
      });
    }

    const editSummary = prevSub
      ? editChanges.length > 0
        ? `تعديل على إرسال ${prevSub.submittedAt}: ${editChanges.join(' · ')}`
        : `إعادة إرسال وتأكيد بعد ${prevSub.submittedAt}`
      : undefined;

    const formattedText = formatAvailabilityChatText(
      branch.name,
      shift,
      tomorrowIso,
      rowsToSend,
      compliance,
      branch.emps
    );

    const newMsgId = `msg_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`;
    const newMsg: ChatMessage = {
      id: newMsgId,
      branchId: branch.id,
      sender: 'branch',
      kind: 'availability',
      text: formattedText,
      shift,
      shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
      submissionKey: key,
      isEditedResend,
      editSummary,
      time: timeNow,
      readByAdmin: false,
      readByBranch: true,
    };

    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      submissions: {
        ...prev.submissions,
        [key]: {
          key,
          branchId: branch.id,
          shift,
          shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
          submittedAt: prevSub ? prevSub.submittedAt : timeNow,
          rows: rowsToSend,
          compliance,
          hasEdit: isEditedResend || prevSub?.hasEdit,
          lastEditAt: isEditedResend ? timeNow : prevSub?.lastEditAt,
          lastEditSummary: editSummary || prevSub?.lastEditSummary,
        },
      },
      messages: [
        ...prev.messages.map((m) =>
          m.branchId === branch.id &&
          m.kind === 'availability' &&
          m.submissionKey === key &&
          !m.supersededByMsgId
            ? { ...m, supersededByMsgId: newMsgId }
            : m
        ),
        newMsg,
      ],
      audit: [
        ...prev.audit,
        {
          id: Date.now(),
          time: timeNow,
          text: isEditedResend
            ? `تعديل توفر الغد (${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}): فرع ${branch.name} — ${editChanges.join(' · ') || 'تحديث'}`
            : isMatched
            ? `إرسال توفر الغد (${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}): فرع ${branch.name} — مطابق للخطة`
            : `إرسال توفر الغد (${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}): فرع ${branch.name} — ${compliance.diffs.length} اختلاف عن الخطة`,
        },
      ],
      notifications: [
        ...prev.notifications,
        {
          id: `ncmp_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`,
          kind: isMatched ? 'compliance_ok' : 'compliance_diff',
          senderRole: 'branch',
          senderName: `فرع ${branch.name}`,
          recipientRole: 'central',
          branchId: branch.id,
          shift,
          shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
          messageId: newMsgId,
          isEdit: isEditedResend,
          text: isEditedResend
            ? `تعديل من فرع ${branch.name} (${SHIFTS_AR[shift]} - غداً): ${editChanges.join('، ') || 'إعادة إرسال'}`
            : isMatched
            ? `إرسال فرع ${branch.name} (${SHIFTS_AR[shift]} - غداً) مطابق للخطة (${compliance.actualCount} متاح)`
            : `تنبيه: إرسال فرع ${branch.name} (${SHIFTS_AR[shift]} - غداً) به ${compliance.diffs.length} اختلاف عن الخطة`,
          time: timeNow,
          read: false,
          complianceReport: compliance,
        },
      ],
    }));

    setConfirmModalOpen(false);
    onChangeSession({ branchScreen: 'home' });
    onNotify(
      isEditedResend
        ? `تم إرسال تعديل ${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}`
        : `تم إرسال توفر ${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}`
    );
  };

  const handleApplyPlanToDraft = () => {
    const plannedRows = buildPlannedRowsForShift(
      state,
      branch.id,
      activeShift,
      tomorrowIso
    );
    setDraftRows(plannedRows);
    onNotify('تم ضبط القائمة حسب الخطة الأسبوعية للغد');
  };

  const handleSendTextMessage = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const txt = chatInput.trim();
    if (!txt) return;
    const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });

    const msgId = `msg_txt_${Date.now()}`;
    const msg: ChatMessage = {
      id: msgId,
      branchId: branch.id,
      sender: 'branch',
      kind: 'text',
      text: txt,
      shift: activeShift,
      shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
      time: timeNow,
      readByAdmin: false,
      readByBranch: true,
    };

    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      messages: [...prev.messages, msg],
      notifications: [
        ...prev.notifications,
        {
          id: `ntxt_${Date.now()}`,
          kind: 'message',
          senderRole: 'branch',
          senderName: `رسالة من فرع ${branch.name}`,
          recipientRole: 'central',
          branchId: branch.id,
          shift: activeShift,
          shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
          messageId: msgId,
          text: `رسالة من فرع ${branch.name}: ${txt}`,
          time: timeNow,
          read: false,
        },
      ],
    }));

    setChatInput('');
  };

  const handleAckAdminMessage = (msg: ChatMessage) => {
    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      messages: prev.messages.map((m) =>
        m.id === msg.id ? { ...m, readByBranch: true } : m
      ),
      transferNotices: prev.transferNotices.map((t) =>
        t.targetBranchId === branch.id && !t.acknowledged
          ? { ...t, acknowledged: true }
          : t
      ),
      notifications: prev.notifications.map((n) =>
        n.recipientRole === 'branch' && n.recipientBranchId === branch.id && !n.read
          ? { ...n, read: true }
          : n
      ),
    }));
    onNotify('تم تأكيد الاطلاع');
  };

  const handleConfirmEmergency = (shift: ShiftType, empId: string) => {
    if (!emergencyReason) return;
    const reasonLabel =
      emergencyReason === 'with_permission' ? 'غياب بإذن' : 'غياب بدون إذن';
    const key = submissionKey(branch.id, shift, tomorrowIso);
    const sub = state.submissions[key];
    if (!sub) return;
    const targetRow = sub.rows.find((r) => r.empId === empId);
    if (!targetRow) return;

    const timeNow = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });

    const nextRows = sub.rows.map((r) =>
      r.empId === empId
        ? { ...r, status: 'unavailable' as const, reason: reasonLabel, emergency: true }
        : r
    );
    const nextCompliance = evaluateAvailabilityCompliance(
      state,
      branch.id,
      shift,
      tomorrowIso,
      nextRows
    );

    const emgMsgId = `msg_emg_${Date.now()}`;
    const emgMsg: ChatMessage = {
      id: emgMsgId,
      branchId: branch.id,
      sender: 'branch',
      kind: 'emergency',
      text: `بلاغ غياب طارئ (${SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]})\nالموظف: ${targetRow.empName}\nالسبب: ${reasonLabel}`,
      shift,
      shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
      time: timeNow,
      readByAdmin: false,
      readByBranch: true,
    };

    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      submissions: {
        ...prev.submissions,
        [key]: {
          ...sub,
          rows: nextRows,
          compliance: nextCompliance,
          hasEdit: true,
          lastEditAt: timeNow,
          lastEditSummary: `بلاغ غياب طارئ: ${targetRow.empName} (${reasonLabel})`,
        },
      },
      messages: [...prev.messages, emgMsg],
      emergencyNotices: [
        ...prev.emergencyNotices,
        {
          id: `emg_${Date.now()}`,
          branchId: branch.id,
          branchName: branch.name,
          shift,
          shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
          employeeId: empId,
          employeeName: targetRow.empName,
          reason: reasonLabel,
          time: timeNow,
          acknowledged: false,
        },
      ],
      notifications: [
        ...prev.notifications,
        {
          id: `nemg_${Date.now()}`,
          kind: 'emergency',
          senderRole: 'branch',
          senderName: `فرع ${branch.name}`,
          recipientRole: 'central',
          branchId: branch.id,
          shift,
          shiftDate: tomorrowIso,
          messageId: emgMsgId,
          isEdit: true,
          text: `تغيير طارئ في فرع ${branch.name} (${SHIFTS_AR[shift]}): ${targetRow.empName} — ${reasonLabel}`,
          time: timeNow,
          read: false,
          complianceReport: nextCompliance,
        },
      ],
      audit: [
        ...prev.audit,
        {
          id: Date.now(),
          time: timeNow,
          text: `تغيير طارئ: ${targetRow.empName} (${branch.name} - ${SHIFTS_AR[shift]}) · ${reasonLabel}`,
        },
      ],
    }));

    setEmergencyEmpId(null);
    setEmergencyReason('');
    setEmergencyOpen(false);
    onNotify(`تم إرسال بلاغ الغياب الطارئ لـ ${targetRow.empName}`);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // SCREEN 1: CLEAN UNCLUTTERED WHATSAPP CHAT
  // ==========================================
  if (branchScreen === 'home') {
    const legacyPendingTransfers = state.transferNotices.filter(
      (t) =>
        t.targetBranchId === branch.id &&
        !t.acknowledged &&
        !branchMessages.some((m) => m.kind === 'transfer' && m.text.includes(t.text))
    );

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white shadow-sm lg:h-[calc(100dvh-5.75rem)]">
        {/* Clean Chat Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-white">
              <Building2 className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21] sm:text-base">
                المدير المركزي · فرع {branch.name}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                غداً {fmtDateAr(tomorrowIso)} · الصباحي:{' '}
                <span className={morningSub ? 'text-[#008069]' : 'text-amber-700'}>
                  {morningSub ? 'أُرسل' : 'بانتظار الإرسال'}
                </span>{' '}
                · المسائي:{' '}
                <span className={eveningSub ? 'text-[#008069]' : 'text-amber-700'}>
                  {eveningSub ? 'أُرسل' : 'بانتظار الإرسال'}
                </span>
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenPlanner}
            className="flex min-h-[36px] items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-[#e9edef]"
          >
            <CalendarDays className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#008069]" />
            <span>خطة الأسبوع</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Airy WhatsApp Chat Thread */}
        <div className="wa-chat-bg min-h-[320px] flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 sm:px-5">
          <div className="flex justify-center">
            <span className="rounded-lg bg-white/90 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#54656f] shadow-xs">
              غداً · {fmtDateAr(tomorrowIso)}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Simple System Welcome Bubble */}
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <div className="max-w-[92%] rounded-xl rounded-tr-sm bg-white px-4 py-3 text-[#111b21] shadow-xs sm:max-w-[75%]">
              <div className="mb-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
                المدير المركزي
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#3b4a54] sm:text-sm">
                أهلاً يا مدير فرع {branch.name}. اختر الوردية من الأسفل لإرسال قائمة حضور الغد،
                أو اكتب أي رسالة هنا إذا احتجت التواصل معنا.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {legacyPendingTransfers.map((t) => (
            <div key={t.id} className="flex justify-end">
              <div className="max-w-[92%] rounded-xl rounded-tr-sm border border-rose-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-[#111b21] shadow-xs sm:max-w-[75%]">
                <div className="mb-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-rose-700">
                  المدير المركزي · {t.typeLabel}
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#111b21]">{t.text}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      onUpdateState((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        transferNotices: prev.transferNotices.map((x) =>
                          x.id === t.id ? { ...x, acknowledged: true } : x
                        ),
                      }))
                    }
                    className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
                  >
                    <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>تم الاطلاع</span>
                  </button>
                  <span className="font-mono-num text-[10px] text-[#667781]">{t.time}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {/* Real Messages */}
          {branchMessages.map((msg) => {
            const isBranchSender = msg.sender === 'branch';
            const isHighlighted = highlightedMsgId === msg.id;

            if (isBranchSender) {
              const isEmergency = msg.kind === 'emergency';
              return (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  ref={(el) => {
                    msgRefs.current[msg.id] = el;
                  }}
                  className="flex justify-start"
                >
                  <div
                    className={`max-w-[92%] rounded-xl rounded-tl-sm px-4 py-3 text-[#111b21] shadow-xs transition-all sm:max-w-[75%] ${
                      isEmergency
                        ? 'border border-rose-300 bg-[#fff1f2]'
                        : msg.supersededByMsgId
                        ? 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#eef7ec] opacity-75'
                        : 'bg-[#d9fdd3]'
                    } ${isHighlighted ? 'ring-3 ring-[#008069]' : ''}`}
                  >
                    {msg.isEditedResend && (
                      <div className="mb-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-amber-950">
                        <div>تعديل بعد الإرسال</div>
                        {msg.editSummary && (
                          <div className="mt-0.5 font-semibold text-amber-900">
                            {msg.editSummary}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {msg.supersededByMsgId && (
                      <div className="mb-1.5 inline-block rounded-full bg-white/80 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#54656f]">
                        نسخة سابقة — تم تعديلها
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {msg.kind === 'availability' && msg.shift && (
                      <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-black/10 pb-1.5">
                        <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
                          {SHIFT_FULL_AR[msg.shift]}
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            onChangeSession({
                              activeShift: msg.shift!,
                              branchScreen: 'submit',
                            })
                          }
                          className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-white/85 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:bg-white"
                        >
                          <Edit3 className="h-3 w-3 text-[#008069]" />
                          <span>تعديل</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="whitespace-pre-line text-xs font-semibold leading-relaxed text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                      {msg.text}
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                      <span className="font-mono-num">{msg.time}</span>
                      <CheckCheck
                        className={`h-4 w-4 ${
                          msg.readByAdmin ? 'text-[#53bdeb]' : 'text-[#667781]'
                        }`}
                      />
                      <span className={msg.readByAdmin ? 'text-[#0284c7]' : 'text-[#667781]'}>
                        {msg.readByAdmin ? 'تم الاطلاع' : 'تم الإرسال'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            }

            // Incoming from Central Admin
            return (
              <div
                key={msg.id}
                ref={(el) => {
                  msgRefs.current[msg.id] = el;
                }}
                className="flex justify-end"
              >
                <div
                  className={`max-w-[92%] rounded-xl rounded-tr-sm bg-white px-4 py-3 text-[#111b21] shadow-xs transition-all sm:max-w-[75%] ${
                    isHighlighted ? 'ring-3 ring-[#008069]' : ''
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="mb-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
                    المدير المركزي{msg.kind === 'transfer' ? ' · قرار نقل موظف' : ''}
                  </div>
                  <div className="whitespace-pre-line text-xs font-semibold leading-relaxed text-[#111b21] sm:text-sm">
                    {msg.text}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    {!msg.readByBranch ? (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleAckAdminMessage(msg)}
                        className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
                      >
                        <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>تم الاطلاع</span>
                      </button>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#008069]">
                        <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        <span>تم الاطلاع</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="font-mono-num text-[10px] text-[#667781]">{msg.time}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}

          <div ref={chatEndRef} />
        </div>

        {/* Compact Uncluttered Bottom Bar */}
        <div className="border-t border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] p-2.5 sm:p-3">
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              id="registerTomorrowBtn_morning"
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                onChangeSession({ activeShift: 'morning', branchScreen: 'submit' })
              }
              className={`flex min-h-[42px] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors sm:text-sm ${
                morningSub
                  ? 'border border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#006e5a]'
                  : 'bg-[#008069] text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]'
              }`}
            >
              <Sun className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{morningSub ? 'تعديل الصباحي' : 'إرسال الصباحي'}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              id="registerTomorrowBtn_evening"
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                onChangeSession({ activeShift: 'evening', branchScreen: 'submit' })
              }
              className={`flex min-h-[42px] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition-colors sm:text-sm ${
                eveningSub
                  ? 'border border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#006e5a]'
                  : 'bg-[#008069] text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]'
              }`}
            >
              <Moon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{eveningSub ? 'تعديل المسائي' : 'إرسال المسائي'}</span>
            </button>

            {submittedForTomorrow.length > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setEmergencyOpen(true)}
                className="flex min-h-[42px] shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-300 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-rose-800 hover:bg-rose-100"
                title="بلاغ غياب طارئ"
              >
                <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">غياب طارئ</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSendTextMessage} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={chatInput}
              onChange={(e) => setChatInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="اكتب رسالة لو عايز..."
              className="min-h-[42px] flex-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-[#111b21] outline-none focus:border-[#008069]"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!chatInput.trim()}
              aria-label="إرسال رسالة"
              className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#008069] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-[#016b58] disabled:bg-[#aebac1]"
            >
              <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Emergency Modal */}
        {emergencyOpen && (
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
            onClick={() => setEmergencyOpen(false)}
          >
            <div
              className="max-h-[85vh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xl"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#e9edef] pb-3">
                <h3 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                  بلاغ غياب طارئ للغد
                </h3>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEmergencyOpen(false)}
                  className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#54656f]"
                >
                  إغلاق
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-4">
                {submittedForTomorrow.map(({ shift, rows }) => (
                  <div key={shift}>
                    <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-extrabold">
                      <span className="text-[#111b21]">{SHIFT_FULL_AR[shift]}</span>
                      <span className="text-[#008069]">
                        متاح: {rows.filter((r) => !r.movedOut && r.status === 'available').length}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {rows.map((r) => (
                        <div
                          key={r.empId}
                          className="rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f8fafc] p-3"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <div>
                              <span
                                className={`text-sm font-bold ${
                                  r.movedOut
                                    ? 'text-[#8696a0] line-through'
                                    : r.status === 'available'
                                    ? 'text-[#111b21]'
                                    : 'text-[#667781]'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {r.empName}
                              </span>
                              {r.note && (
                                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-amber-700">{r.note}</p>
                              )}
                            </div>

                            {r.movedOut ? null : r.status === 'available' ? (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                  setEmergencyReason('');
                                  setEmergencyEmpId((cur) =>
                                    cur === r.empId ? null : r.empId
                                  );
                                }}
                                className="rounded-full bg-rose-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-rose-800 hover:bg-rose-200"
                              >
                                غياب طارئ
                              </button>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-700">
                                {r.emergency ? 'طارئ: ' : 'غائب: '}
                                {r.reason || ''}
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {emergencyEmpId === r.empId &&
                            r.status === 'available' &&
                            !r.movedOut && (
                              <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 border-t border-[#e9edef] pt-2.5">
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setEmergencyReason('with_permission')}
                                    className={`rounded-full py-2 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                                      emergencyReason === 'with_permission'
                                        ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                                        : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#111b21]'
                                    }`}
                                  >
                                    غياب بإذن
                                  </button>
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setEmergencyReason('without_permission')}
                                    className={`rounded-full py-2 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                                      emergencyReason === 'without_permission'
                                        ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                                        : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#111b21]'
                                    }`}
                                  >
                                    غياب بدون إذن
                                  </button>
                                </div>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  disabled={!emergencyReason}
                                  onClick={() => handleConfirmEmergency(shift, r.empId)}
                                  className="w-full rounded-full bg-rose-700 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white disabled:opacity-45"
                                >
                                  إرسال البلاغ في المحادثة
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ==========================================
  // SCREEN 2: AVAILABILITY CHECKLIST SHEET
  // ==========================================
  if (branchScreen === 'submit') {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white shadow-sm lg:h-[calc(100dvh-5.75rem)]">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-3">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
              تحضير {SHIFT_FULL_AR[activeShift]}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#54656f]">
              غداً ({fmtDateAr(tomorrowIso)}) · {SHIFT_START_DESC[activeShift]}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-0.5">
              {(['morning', 'evening'] as ShiftType[]).map((sh) => (
                <button
                  key={sh}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onChangeSession({ activeShift: sh })}
