                  className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                    activeShift === sh
                      ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                      : 'text-[#54656f] hover:text-[#111b21]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {SHIFTS_AR[sh]}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onChangeSession({ branchScreen: 'home' })}
              className="flex min-h-[38px] items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21]"
            >
              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>للمحادثة</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="wa-chat-bg flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 sm:px-5">
          <div
            id="compliancePreviewBox"
            className="rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-3.5 shadow-xs"
          >
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="text-xs font-extrabold">
                {liveCompliance.status === 'matched' ? (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#008069]">
                    <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>القائمة مطابقة لخطة الغد الأسبوعية</span>
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-800">
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>يوجد {liveCompliance.diffs.length} اختلاف عن خطة الغد</span>
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              <button
                id="autoFillFromPlanBtn"
                type="button"
                onClick={handleApplyPlanToDraft}
                className="flex min-h-[42px] items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#008069] bg-[#e7fce3] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#006e5a] hover:bg-[#d9fdd3]"
              >
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                <span>تعبئة حسب خطة الغد</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {draftRows.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-8 text-center text-sm text-[#54656f] shadow-xs">
              لا يوجد موظفون مسجلون في هذه الوردية. أضفهم من «طاقم الفرع» أو «خطة الأسبوع».
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {draftRows.map((row) => {
                const planItem = tomorrowPlan.find((p) => p.emp.id === row.empId);
                const isPlannedOff = planItem?.plannedShift === 'off';
                const isBetween =
                  planItem?.plannedShift === 'between' || planItem?.emp.shift === 'between';

                return (
                  <div
                    key={row.empId}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-[#e9edef] bg-white p-4 shadow-xs"
                  >
                    <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <div
                          className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold ${
                            row.status === 'available'
                              ? 'bg-[#e7fce3] text-[#008069]'
                              : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {row.empName.slice(0, 1)}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                            {row.empName}
                          </span>
                          {isBetween && (
                            <span className="rounded-md bg-amber-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-amber-900">
                              بتوين ({activeShift === 'morning' ? 'من 10:00 ص' : 'حتى 11:00 م'})
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {planItem && (
                        <span
                          className={`rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                            isPlannedOff
                              ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700'
                              : 'bg-[#f0f2f5] text-[#54656f]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isPlannedOff
                            ? `خطة الغد: غير متاح (${planItem.reason})`
                            : `خطة الغد: ${SHIFTS_AR[activeShift]}`}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setDraftRows((prev) =>
                            prev.map((x) =>
                              x.empId === row.empId
                                ? { ...x, status: 'available', reason: null }
                                : x
                            )
                          )
                        }
                        className={`flex min-h-[46px] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl py-2.5 text-sm font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                          row.status === 'available'
                            ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                            : 'bg-[#f0f2f5] text-[#54656f] hover:bg-[#e9edef]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                        <span>حضور</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          setDraftRows((prev) =>
                            prev.map((x) =>
                              x.empId === row.empId
                                ? {
                                    ...x,
                                    status: 'unavailable',
                                    reason: x.reason || 'إجازة رسمية',
                                  }
                                : x
                            )
                          )
                        }
                        className={`flex min-h-[46px] flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl py-2.5 text-sm font-extrabold transition-colors ${
                          row.status === 'unavailable'
                            ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                            : 'bg-[#f0f2f5] text-[#54656f] hover:bg-[#e9edef]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                        <span>غير متاح</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {row.status === 'unavailable' && (
                      <div className="mt-3 border-t border-[#e9edef] pt-2.5">
                        <div className="mb-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#54656f]">
                          اختر السبب بضغطة واحدة:
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                          {UNAVAILABLE_REASONS.map((rs) => (
                            <button
                              key={rs}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() =>
                                setDraftRows((prev) =>
                                  prev.map((x) =>
                                    x.empId === row.empId ? { ...x, reason: rs } : x
                                  )
                                )
                              }
                              className={`min-h-[34px] rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold ${
                                row.reason === rs
                                  ? 'bg-rose-700 text-white'
                                  : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] text-[#3b4a54]'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {rs}
                            </button>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {draftRows.length > 0 && (
          <div className="border-t border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] p-3.5">
            <div className="mb-2 flex justify-between text-sm font-extrabold">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[#008069]">
                <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>حضور: {availCount}</span>
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-rose-700">
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>غير متاح: {unavailCount}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              id="sendAvailabilityBtn"
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setConfirmModalOpen(true)}
              className="flex min-h-[52px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-3 text-base font-extrabold text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-[#016b58]"
            >
              <Send className="h-5 w-5" />
              <span>إرسال توفر الغد</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {confirmModalOpen && (
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
            onClick={() => setConfirmModalOpen(false)}
          >
            <div
              className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <h3 className="mb-3 text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
                تأكيد إرسال التوفر؟
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-1.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#3b4a54]">
                <p className="text-[#008069]">حضور: {availCount}</p>
                <p className="text-rose-700">غير متاح: {unavailCount}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmModalOpen(false)}
                  className="min-h-[44px] flex-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#3b4a54]"
                >
                  تعديل
                </button>
                <button
                  id="confirmSendBtn"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => dispatchAvailabilitySubmission(activeShift, draftRows)}
                  className="min-h-[44px] flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
                >
                  إرسال الآن
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ==========================================
  // SCREEN 3: DEDICATED CENTERED STAFF & LEAVES VIEW
  // ==========================================
  const handleAddBranchEmp = () => {
    const name = newName.trim();
    if (!name) {
      onNotify('أدخل اسم الموظف');
      return;
    }

    const monthlyLeaves: MonthlyLeave[] = [];
    if (newMonthlyDraft) {
      const refMonth = pad2(cairoToday.month + 1);
      const refDay = pad2(newMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth);
      monthlyLeaves.push({
        id: `ml_${Date.now()}`,
        recurringDayOfMonth: newMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth,
        startDate: `${cairoToday.year}-${refMonth}-${refDay}`,
        daysCount: newMonthlyDraft.daysCount,
        skipWeeklyInSameWeek: newMonthlyDraft.skipWeeklyInSameWeek,
      });
    }

    const emp: Employee = {
      id: `e_${branch.id}_${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      shift: newShift,
      suspended: false,
      weeklyOffDay: newOffDay,
      extraOffDay1: null,
      extraOffDay2: null,
      monthlyLeaves,
    };

    onUpdateState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
        b.id === branch.id ? { ...b, emps: [...b.emps, emp] } : b
      ),
    }));
    setNewName('');
    setNewMonthlyDraft(null);
    onNotify(`تمت إضافة ${name} لطاقم الفرع`);
  };

  const weeklyDialEmp =
    weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'emp'
      ? branch.emps.find((e) => e.id === weeklyDialTarget.empId) || null
      : null;
  const monthlyDialEmp =
    monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'emp'
      ? branch.emps.find((e) => e.id === monthlyDialTarget.empId) || null
      : null;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white px-4 py-3 shadow-xs">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
            طاقم فرع {branch.name}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#54656f]">
            إضافة موظف وتحديد إجازته الأسبوعية والشهرية بالدائرية
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onChangeSession({ branchScreen: 'home' })}
          className="flex min-h-[38px] items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21]"
        >
          <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>عودة للمحادثة</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white p-5 shadow-xs">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
          إضافة موظف جديد مع إجازته
        </h2>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="اسم الموظف"
            value={newName}
            onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)}
            className="min-h-[44px] w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-bold outline-none focus:border-[#008069]"
          />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            {(['morning', 'between', 'evening'] as ShiftType[]).map((sh) => (
              <button
                key={sh}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setNewShift(sh)}
                className={`min-h-[40px] rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold ${
                  newShift === sh
                    ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                    : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] text-[#3b4a54]'
                }`}
              >
                {SHIFTS_AR[sh]}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setWeeklyDialTarget({ mode: 'new' })}
              className="flex min-h-[44px] items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:border-[#008069]"
            >
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#54656f]">
                <Clock className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                <span>الأسبوعية:</span>
              </span>
              <span className="rounded-full bg-[#008069] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-white">
                {newOffDay === null ? 'بدون' : DAY_NAMES_AR[newOffDay]}
              </span>
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setMonthlyDialTarget({ mode: 'new' })}
                className="flex min-h-[44px] flex-1 items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#111b21] hover:border-[#008069]"
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#54656f]">
                  <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4 text-[#008069]" />
                  <span>شهرية:</span>
                </span>
                <span
                  className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 ${
                    newMonthlyDraft
                      ? 'bg-[#008069] text-white'
                      : 'border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white text-[#54656f]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {newMonthlyDraft
                    ? `يوم ${newMonthlyDraft.dayOfMonth} (${newMonthlyDraft.daysCount} يوم)`
                    : 'تحديد بالدائرية'}
                </span>
              </button>
              {newMonthlyDraft && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setNewMonthlyDraft(null)}
                  className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700"
                  aria-label="مسح الإجازة الشهرية"
                >
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleAddBranchEmp}
            className="flex min-h-[44px] w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#008069] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-white hover:bg-[#016b58]"
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>إضافة للطاقم</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d1d7db] bg-white shadow-xs">
        <div className="border-b border-[#e9edef] bg-[#f0f2f5] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">
          موظفو الفرع ({branch.emps.filter((e) => !e.suspended).length})
        </div>
        <div className="divide-y divide-[#e9edef]">
          {branch.emps
            .filter((e) => !e.suspended)
            .map((emp) => (
              <div
                key={emp.id}
                className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-3 text-sm"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#e7fce3] text-sm font-extrabold text-[#008069]">
                    {emp.name.slice(0, 1)}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-extrabold text-[#111b21]">{emp.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#54656f]">
                      {SHIFTS_AR[emp.shift]} · أسبوعية:{' '}
                      {emp.weeklyOffDay === null ? 'بدون' : DAY_NAMES_AR[emp.weeklyOffDay]}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setWeeklyDialTarget({ mode: 'emp', empId: emp.id })}
                    className="rounded-full border border-[#d1d7db] bg-[#f8fafc] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#111b21] hover:border-[#008069]"
                  >
                    الأسبوعية
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setMonthlyDialTarget({ mode: 'emp', empId: emp.id })}
                    className="rounded-full border border-[#008069] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#008069] hover:bg-[#e7fce3]"
                  >
                    شهرية ({(emp.monthlyLeaves || []).length})
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      onUpdateState((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
                          b.id === branch.id
                            ? { ...b, emps: b.emps.filter((x) => x.id !== emp.id) }
                            : b
                        ),
                      }));
                      onNotify(`تم حذف ${emp.name}`);
                    }}
                    className="rounded-full border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-2 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100"
                    aria-label={`حذف ${emp.name}`}
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <WeeklyOffDialModal
        open={weeklyDialTarget !== null}
        selectedDay={
          weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newOffDay
            : weeklyDialEmp?.weeklyOffDay ?? null
        }
        onCancel={() => setWeeklyDialTarget(null)}
        onConfirm={(pickedDay) => {
          if (weeklyDialTarget?.mode === 'new') {
            setNewOffDay(pickedDay);
          } else if (weeklyDialEmp) {
            onUpdateState((prev) => ({
              ...prev,
              branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
                b.id !== branch.id
                  ? b
                  : {
                      ...b,
                      emps: b.emps.map((e) =>
                        e.id === weeklyDialEmp.id ? { ...e, weeklyOffDay: pickedDay } : e
                      ),
                    }
              ),
            }));
            onNotify(`تم تحديث الإجازة الأسبوعية لـ ${weeklyDialEmp.name}`);
          }
          setWeeklyDialTarget(null);
        }}
      />

      <MonthlyLeaveDialModal
        open={monthlyDialTarget !== null}
        initialDayOfMonth={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new' ? newMonthlyDraft?.dayOfMonth ?? 5 : 5
        }
        initialDaysCount={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new' ? newMonthlyDraft?.daysCount ?? 2 : 2
        }
        initialSkipWeekly={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newMonthlyDraft?.skipWeeklyInSameWeek ?? true
            : true
        }
        employeeName={
          monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new'
            ? newName.trim() || 'موظف جديد'
            : monthlyDialEmp?.name
        }
        onCancel={() => setMonthlyDialTarget(null)}
        onConfirm={(res) => {
          if (monthlyDialTarget?.mode === 'new') {
            setNewMonthlyDraft(res);
          } else if (monthlyDialEmp) {
            const refMonth = pad2(cairoToday.month + 1);
            const refDay = pad2(res.dayOfMonth);
            const newLeave: MonthlyLeave = {
              id: `ml_${Date.now()}`,
              recurringDayOfMonth: res.dayOfMonth,
              startDate: `${cairoToday.year}-${refMonth}-${refDay}`,
              daysCount: res.daysCount,
              skipWeeklyInSameWeek: res.skipWeeklyInSameWeek,
            };
            onUpdateState((prev) => ({
              ...prev,
              branches: prev.branches.map((b) =>
                b.id !== branch.id
                  ? b
                  : {
                      ...b,
                      emps: b.emps.map((e) =>
                        e.id === monthlyDialEmp.id
                          ? { ...e, monthlyLeaves: [...(e.monthlyLeaves || []), newLeave] }
                          : e
                      ),
                    }
              ),
            }));
            onNotify(
              `تم حفظ إجازة ${monthlyDialEmp.name}: يوم ${res.dayOfMonth} كل شهر (${res.daysCount} يوم)`
            );
          }
          setMonthlyDialTarget(null);
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================
FILE: src/components/AdminControlView.tsx
